### [60] [Reentrance and temperature chaos in the $p$-spin Ising spin glass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23904)
*Hidetoshi Nishimori*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文在全连接的伊辛p-自旋玻璃模型中，通过自由能在三相点附近的展开，解析地证明了铁磁-自旋玻璃相界在三相点附近是可重入的，并由此推导出自旋玻璃相中存在温度混沌现象，即不同温度下的自旋构型重叠消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究自旋玻璃中的可重入转变与温度混沌之间的逻辑关系，并提供具体的物理模型支持。

Method: 通过在三相点附近展开自由能，分析铁磁-自旋玻璃相界的可重入性，并结合两温度副本计算验证温度混沌的存在。

Result: 发现在有限p>2范围内，铁磁-自旋玻璃相界在三相点附近具有可重入性；进一步表明自旋玻璃相中至少存在一对温度使得自旋构型重叠为零，强烈暗示在整个自旋玻璃相中普遍存在温度混沌。

Conclusion: 可重入转变与温度混沌在该模型中存在内在联系，结果支持自旋玻璃相中广泛存在温度混沌的结论。

Abstract: Reentrant transitions and temperature chaos are two unusual properties of spin glasses and had long been considered physically unrelated. We recently established a logical relation between these two phenomena [Phys. Rev. E 112, 044140 (2025)]. In the present paper, we provide an explicit example of this logical relation in the fully connected Ising $p$-spin glass with a ferromagnetic bias. By expanding the free energy around the triple point, we show analytically that the ferromagnetic--spin glass boundary is reentrant in the vicinity of the triple point throughout the examined range of finite $p>2$. It then follows from the above logical relation that there exists at least one pair of distinct temperatures in the spin glass phase for which the overlap of spin configurations vanishes. This is a necessary condition for temperature chaos, but it would be quite unusual for the overlap to vanish only for selected temperature pairs but not for others within a single spin glass phase. These results therefore strongly suggest the existence of temperature chaos in the sense that the overlaps of spin configurations vanish for all temperature pairs throughout the spin glass phase. Independent evidence for this behavior is provided by a two-temperature replica calculation.

</details>


### [61] [Arithmetic Tuning of Dynamical Critical Exponents in Quasiperiodic Localization Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23990)
*Tian-Cheng Yi,Yi-Fan Liu,Enguo Guan,Wen-Long You*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 通过调节准周期系统中无理数频率的算术结构，可在不改变哈密顿量形式的情况下调控局域化转变的动力学临界行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究准周期系统中局域化相变的临界指数和普适类，揭示无理数频率对动力学临界行为的影响。

Method: 利用广义斐波那契序列构造受控于(m,n)参数的不可通约频率族，并结合广义保真度 susceptibility、局域长度标度和有限尺寸能隙分析进行数值研究。

Result: 关联长度指数ν≈1与标准Aubry-André-Harper模型一致，不随频率变化；而动力学指数则随无理数频率系统性变化。

Conclusion: 无理数的算术结构可作为调控非平衡量子动力学和临界行为的有效控制参数，无需修改微观哈密顿量或空间维度。

Abstract: The critical exponents and universality classes of localization transitions in quasiperiodic systems are of fundamental importance for understanding critical phenomena in aperiodic systems. Here we show that the dynamical critical behavior can be tuned without adding new terms or changing the form of the Hamiltonian, but solely by varying the incommensurate frequency of the quasiperiodic onsite potential. We construct a family of incommensurate frequencies from the limiting ratios of generalized Fibonacci sequences controlled by the parameters $(m,n)$, and use them to define the quasiperiodic onsite potential. By combining generalized fidelity susceptibility, localization-length scaling, and finite-size gap analysis, we find that the correlation-length exponent is insensitive to the choice of the incommensurate frequency and remains consistent with the correlation-length critical exponent, $ν\simeq 1$, in the localization transition of the standard Aubry--Andr'e--Harper model. In contrast, the dynamical exponent extracted from the low-energy gap scaling varies systematically with the incommensurate frequency. Our results show that changing the incommensurate frequency provides a simple way to tune dynamical critical scaling in deterministic aperiodic systems. Our results suggest instead that the arithmetic structure of an irrational number can serve as a control parameter for nonequilibrium quantum dynamics, enabling the tuning of dynamical critical behavior without changing the microscopic Hamiltonian or the physical spatial dimension.

</details>


### [62] [Disorder-induced conducting edges on Kagomé lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24472)
*A. Chmeruk,D. Jones,L. Chioncel*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 在Kagome晶格系统中，通过簇扩展的相干势近似发现无序平均导致非局域自能，进而调节对角和非对角跃迁项，驱动系统经历拓扑平凡绝缘态到中间无能隙相的转变，并展现出边缘导电模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无序对Kagome晶格系统拓扑性质的影响，探索多重散射效应是否能诱导有效的自旋-轨道相互作用。

Method: 采用簇扩展的相干势近似方法进行无序平均，计算非局域自能及其对跃迁项的重正化效应。

Result: 发现最近邻和次近邻跃迁幅度的重正化可驱动系统在两个拓扑平凡绝缘态之间出现中间无能隙相，并伴随广泛的边缘导电模式。

Conclusion: 多重散射效应本身足以产生有效的自旋-轨道相互作用，并显著改变无序Kagome系统的边缘谱结构。

Abstract: Within a cluster extension of the coherent potential approximation, disorder averaging generates a non-local self-energy that renormalizes both diagonal and off-diagonal hopping terms of the non-interacting Kagome-lattice Hamiltonian. These renormalizations (of both nearest- and next-nearest-neighbor hopping amplitudes) drive the system between two topologically trivial insulating states through an intermediate gapless phase characterized by conducting edge modes over a broad range of impurity concentrations. Our results demonstrate that multiple-scattering effects alone can generate emergent effective spin-orbit interactions and qualitatively modify the edge spectrum of disordered Kagome systems.

</details>

### [63] [A unified dynamical modeling framework for cruise control and adaptive cruise control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23827)
*Mingfeng Shang,Shian Wang*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 提出了一种统一的巡航控制（CC）和自适应巡航控制（ACC）动力学模型，通过设计模式切换来改善交通流，发现商用ACC车辆过大的切换阈值可能是其对交通流产生负面影响的原因，适当降低该阈值可显著提升道路 throughput 和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多聚焦于修改ACC控制算法本身，而较少关注CC与ACC模式之间的切换机制，导致对ACC车辆影响交通流的理解不完整。因此，本文旨在填补这一空白，探索不改变ACC算法的情况下如何优化模式切换以改善交通性能。

Method: 提出一个基于Sigmoid加权函数连续插值CC与ACC模式的统一动力学模型，并进行车队的平衡态与弦稳定性分析；在此基础上设计不同优先级（通量优先与安全优先）下的最优模式切换阈值，并与商用ACC车辆采用的阈值进行对比。

Result: 数值实验表明，通过合理设计切换阈值，道路通行能力最高可提升58.6%，平均速度波动降低达39.7%；分析揭示了安全性、道路通量与弦稳定性之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 商用ACC车辆过大的切换阈值很可能是其恶化交通流的原因，适当减小该阈值可在不更改ACC控制算法的前提下显著改善交通流的稳定性和通行效率。

Abstract: Adaptive cruise control (ACC) vehicles are the first generation of automated vehicles. While fully automated vehicles are expected to benefit traffic flow, field experiments have shown that commercially available ACC vehicles may instead degrade it by reducing string stability and roadway throughput. To mitigate these effects, existing studies adjust the ACC control algorithm or introduce additional control inputs; however, few have examined the transition between the cruise control (CC) and ACC modes without modifying the ACC control algorithm itself, leaving the impacts of ACC vehicles incompletely understood. To address this gap, we propose a unified dynamical model of CC and ACC that interpolates continuously between the two modes through a sigmoid weighting function, and improve traffic flow by designing the mode switching. Based on this new model, we conduct an equilibrium and string stability analysis of the platoon, revealing the trade-off among safety, throughput, and string stability. The optimal switching threshold is designed under throughput-priority and safety-priority criteria, and compared against the threshold adopted by commercially available ACC vehicles. Numerical experiments show that, with a properly designed switching threshold, the throughput increases by up to 58.6% and the average speed variation, a measure of speed oscillations, decreases by up to 39.7% relative to the commercial baseline. We conclude that the excessively large switching threshold of commercially available ACC vehicles is a likely cause of their negative impact on traffic flow, and that this impact can be mitigated by properly reducing the threshold toward a safer and more string-stable regime.

</details>


### [64] [Small-World Communication Fabrics for Neuromorphic Multicore-SoCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24351)
*Sebastian Billaudelle,Christian Metzner,Jimmy Weber,Zhe Su,Chenxi Wen,Siqi Liu,Laura Kriener,Filippo Moro,Giacomo Indiveri,Melika Payvand*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文比较了两种基于22nm FDSOI技术的多核神经形态系统（NeoCorAl与MOSAIC）在互连通信中的路由架构差异，分析其在灵活性、延迟、内存和可扩展性方面的权衡，并探讨通信局部性对性能的影响及路由感知训练的优化潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着神经形态系统规模扩大，核间事件通信成为内存、延迟和能耗的主要瓶颈；受生物神经系统小世界组织启发，需设计高效的片上通信架构以应对扩展性挑战。

Method: 对比两种在相同22nm FDSOI工艺下实现且针对稀疏长程连接优化的多核系统：NeoCorAl采用异步分组交换树结构支持层次化组播，MOSAIC则使用基于RRAM的电路交换二维网格，在内存中进行路由。通过分析空间嵌入、随机和分层网络中的通信模式，评估其在路由灵活性、跳数、内存需求、组播效率和可扩展性方面的表现。

Result: 两种架构在不同通信局部性场景下各有优劣：树形结构在组播效率和内存开销方面更优，而网格结构在路由灵活性和可扩展性上更具潜力；具体性能依赖于网络拓扑特性。

Conclusion: 路由架构的选择应结合应用的通信模式，未来可通过路由感知训练协同优化神经连接性、任务性能与硬件映射效率，从而提升整体系统效能。

Abstract: As neuromorphic systems scale beyond a single core, inter-core event communication can become a dominant contributor to memory footprint, latency, and energy consumption. Biological neural systems address a similar scaling challenge through small-world organization, combining dense local connectivity with sparse long-range projections. In this work, we compare two recent multicore neuromorphic systems implemented in the same 22-nm FDSOI technology and explicitly optimized for such connectivity. The first, NeoCorAl, uses an asynchronous packet-switched tree with hierarchical multicast, whereas the second, MOSAIC, employs an RRAM-based, circuit-switched two-dimensional mesh that performs routing in memory. We examine the resulting trade-offs in routing flexibility, hop count, memory requirements, multicast efficiency, and scalability. We further study how the relative efficiency of tree- and mesh-based routing depends on communication locality in spatially-embedded, random, and layered networks. Finally, we discuss routing-aware training as a means of jointly optimizing neural connectivity, task performance, and hardware mappability.

</details>


### [65] [HORIZON: A Read-Efficient Firmware for DNA Storage with Horizontal Layout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24839)
*Alex Sensintaffar,Roop Kiran,Yang Chen,Mai Zheng,Bingzhe Li*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: HORIZON是一种用于DNA存储的读取高效分配策略，通过活动感知的水平放置减少读取放大，相比现有方案显著提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的DNA存储随机访问设计由于顺序布局导致频繁和不频繁访问的数据共存于同一引物对下，引起高读取放大问题。

Method: HORIZON采用轮转方式将写入数据分布到不同的引物对上，并基于块活动性和引物对占用情况分配数据，同时使用滑动窗口温度模型跟踪最近的访问频率。

Result: 模拟结果显示，HORIZON在MSR和FIU traces及合成文件系统工作负载下， consistently 降低了读取放大。

Conclusion: HORIZON通过活动感知的水平布局有效减少了DNA存储中的读取放大，提高了读取效率。

Abstract: DNA storage is a promising medium for long-term archiving, but its read performance is limited by coarse-grained random access. Existing random-access DNA storage designs suffer from high read amplification because their sequential layouts co-locate frequently and infrequently accessed data under the same primer pair, where any read must retrieve all associated strands even when only a small fraction is needed. We present HORIZON, a read-efficient allocation policy for DNA block devices that reduces read amplification through activity-aware horizontal placement. HORIZON first introduces a horizontal layout distributing writes round-robin across primer pairs, rather than filling each sequentially. It classifies newly written blocks in the write buffer as active or inactive, tracks recent primer-pair accesses using a sliding-window temperature model, and allocates blocks based on block activity and primer-pair occupancy. Simulations show HORIZON consistently reduces read amplification compared with state-of-the-art schemes across MSR and FIU traces and synthetic filesystem workloads.

</details>

### [66] [Macroparticles with different weights relax to different temperatures in Particle-In-Cell simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23894)
*Remi Lehe,Arianna Formenti,Justin R. Angus,Jean-Luc Vay*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了粒子模拟中不同权重宏粒子导致的非物理热平衡问题，提出了预测温度演化的方程，并给出了延迟该非物理平衡建立的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在粒子模拟中，不同权重的宏粒子可能导致非物理的热平衡，影响模拟结果的准确性，因此需要研究其机制并提出解决方案。

Method: 通过分析PIC算法中的有效碰撞性，推导出预测温度时间演化的方程，并与PIC模拟结果进行对比验证。

Result: 发现低权重宏粒子温度偏高，高权重宏粒子温度偏低，推导的方程能准确预测温度演化过程。

Conclusion: 该非物理热平衡虽难以避免但发展较慢，可通过调整算法参数延缓其出现，从而提高模拟的可靠性。

Abstract: A distinctive feature of Particle-In-Cell (PIC) simulations is the use of macroparticles, each representing many physical particles. The associated macroparticle weight can vary from one species to another, or even from one macroparticle to another. Here we show that, when macroparticles have different weights, the plasma evolves toward an unphysical thermal equilibrium in which the temperatures are unequal: lower-weight macroparticles reach a higher temperature than physically expected, and higher-weight macroparticles a lower one. We show that this unphysical equilibrium is difficult to avoid, but that reaching it takes time, and that the associated timescale depends on the effective collisionality of the PIC algorithm. We derive an equation that predicts the full time evolution of the temperatures, in good agreement with PIC simulations, and use it to identify strategies to delay the establishment of this unphysical thermal equilibrium.

</details>


### [67] [A sharp-diffuse interface model for intermittent and isolated topological transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24030)
*Raaghav Ramani*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合锐-弥散界面表示方法，用于模拟Cahn-Hilliard相分离过程中的间歇性和孤立拓扑转变，通过局部化弥散演化显著提高计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了高效模拟在Cahn-Hilliard相粗化过程中发生的拓扑结构变化，同时保持界面演化的准确性。

Result: 实现了二维数值模拟，展示了界面合并过程中的质量交换问题，相较于传统弥散界面模拟获得了两到三个数量级的加速。

Conclusion: 该方法通过将弥散界面演化局限于拓扑事件，显著提升了计算效率，同时保持了对复杂界面动力学的准确描述。

Abstract: We propose a hybrid sharp-diffuse interface representation for modeling intermittent and isolated topological transitions during Cahn-Hilliard phase coarsening. Away from topological events, the evolution is approximated by the Mullins-Sekerka sharp-interface limit system and computed using a boundary integral formulation. When diffuse transition layers overlap, a novel interface surgery algorithm resolves topology changes through a localized Cahn-Hilliard pseudo-time evolution, after which the sharp-interface calculation is resumed. We develop the mathematical formulation underlying this decomposition, present a simple two-dimensional numerical implementation, and simulate a mass-exchange problem with interface coalescence. By localizing the diffuse evolution to topological events, the method achieves a speedup of two to three orders of magnitude over conventional diffuse-interface simulations.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 研究基于纽约市和多伦多的地理定位AED数据、急救报告的心脏骤停事件以及网约车出行数据，构建并分析移动AED模型，在总AED数量固定的条件下，评估不同比例的静态与移动部署（包括混合策略）对响应时间和覆盖可靠性的影响。

Result: 在纽约市，当55%至70%的AED为移动式时，平均响应时间缩短超过2.5分钟；超出该范围则因静态覆盖不足而导致效益下降。在多伦多，平均响应时间改善接近4分钟，并在趋向全移动配置时趋于稳定。

Conclusion: 移动AED能显著提升急救响应效率和可靠性，但最佳的静态与移动AED配比需根据城市的覆盖状况和交通流动性特征进行本地化优化。

Abstract: Timely access to automated external defibrillators (AEDs) remains a major limitation of public-access defibrillation systems because stationary AED networks cannot adapt to spatial and temporal variation in out-of-hospital cardiac arrest (OHCA) demand. We study a platform-enabled mobile AED model in which AEDs are carried by existing urban ride-hailing fleets that can be dispatched to OHCA locations. Rather than assuming additional AED capacity, we hold the total AED supply fixed and compare alternative allocations between stationary and mobile deployment, including hybrid policies that retain both forms of coverage. Our analysis leverages real-world urban data from New York City and Toronto, integrating georeferenced public AED inventories, EMS-reported OHCA incident data, and ride-hailing mobility data. The results show that mobile AEDs can substantially improve both response times and reliability, but the benefits depend on how AED capacity is divided between stationary and mobile deployment. In New York City, average response-time gains exceed 2.5 minutes and peak when approximately 55 to 70 percent of AED capacity is mobile; beyond this range, both gains and reliability decline as stationary coverage is reduced. In Toronto, average gains approach 4 minutes and then plateau as the system moves toward a fully mobile configuration. These contrasting patterns show that mobile AEDs can materially improve access, but the best mix of stationary and mobile AEDs must be tailored to local coverage and mobility conditions.

</details>


### [70] [Dimensionless Controls of Plasticity Under Alternating Tasks: From Evolutionary Biology to Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23889)
*Owen Skriloff*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度学习中网络在交替学习两个布尔标签集时的可塑性，发现系统可简化为两个无量纲控制参数：任务分歧度 $r$ 和学习率与切换周期的乘积 $ηT$。这两个参数共同决定了可塑性的上下界，并揭示了最优 $ηT$ 与 $r$ 之间的近似反幂律关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受基因型-表型映射研究的启发，探索进化生物学中的可塑性机制在梯度下降学习中是否依然适用。

Method: 构建一个深度学习模型，在两个布尔标签集之间交替训练，将生物系统中提出的四个因素重新解释为训练动力学中的变量，并通过大量实验轨迹进行验证。

Result: 系统简化为两个关键参数 $r$ 和 $ηT$；$r$ 决定了乌托邦距离的几何下限，二者共同约束遗忘程度；ANOVA分析显示 $r$、$η$、$T$ 主导可塑性，而中性集大小影响可忽略；最优 $ηT^*$ 与 $r$ 呈近似反幂律关系 $ηT^{*}\propto r^{-1.18}$。

Conclusion: 从生物类比到深度学习，幸存下来的并非几何结构而是动力学机制，该框架为理解可塑性提供了类似物理和工程中驱动系统的视角。

Abstract: Plasticity under changing environments is central to both evolutionary biology and continual learning. Motivated by recent work on genotype--phenotype maps, we study a minimal deep-learning analogue where a network is trained alternately on two Boolean label sets, and ask which biological controls of plasticity survive the translation to gradient descent. Reinterpreting four proposed biological factors as quantities of training dynamics, we find the system reduces to two dimensionless controls: the task disagreement $r$, the fraction of disagreeing labels, and the reach $ηT$, the product of learning rate and switching period. We derive two bounds on plasticity: $r$ alone fixes an extremal geometric floor on the utopia distance, while $r$ and $ηT$ jointly bound forgetting. Across 9,720 trajectories, an ANOVA confirms that $r$, $η$, and $T$ dominate, while the effect of neutral-set size (emphasized in the biological setting) is negligible. The optimal reach itself follows an approximate inverse power law $ηT^{*}\propto r^{-1.18}$, yielding a heuristic that sets the optimal reach $ηT^*$ from the task disagreement alone. The analogy that survives is therefore dynamical rather than geometric, and our setting enables a view of plasticity through the lens of other driven systems in physics and engineering.

</details>


### [71] [The Shadow Price of Intelligence: Quality Degradation in LLM Inference as a Supply Chain Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23986)
*Elioth Sanabria*

TL;DR: 本文指出大型语言模型提供商在计算资源受限时通过降低服务质量来应对拥塞，但这种做法忽视了答案失败导致的重试和用户流失，实际可能增加系统负载并破坏长期价值。作者提出新的建模方法，揭示降级策略在容量紧张时反而可能消耗更多资源，并强调节流本质上是需求调控而非成本控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行业做法在模型拥塞时降级服务以节省成本，但忽略了失败回答引发的重试和用户流失对系统和商业价值的长期影响，需要重新评估其经济合理性。

Method: 通过建立包含客户终身价值流失、几何重试乘数和双机制瞬态队列的理论模型，分析推理资源分配；引入影子价格和最优配给策略，结合静态与动态分析及随机性扩展。

Result: 发现使用更小模型虽单次成本低，但因失败率高导致重试增多，在容量紧张时反而消耗更多资源；动态节流可能触发‘点燃阈值’，制造比缓解更多的流量；最优策略应按客户类别和时段差异化分配智能资源。

Conclusion: 节流不仅是成本管理工具，更是影响需求的关键杠杆；在拥塞情况下，错误的节流策略会恶化系统负载并损害长期价值，应采用基于影子价格的精细化资源分配政策。

Abstract: Large language model providers are compute constrained, and their universal response to congestion is to degrade service: route queries to smaller models, cut reasoning effort, truncate context. The industry's accounting says this saves money. We show the accounting is wrong, because it prices a query when the customer buys an answer. A degraded answer fails with some probability, and a failed answer either returns as a retry, inflating arrivals when the system is most loaded, or departs as churn, destroying lifetime value on a ledger no cost dashboard displays. We model inference allocation with three classical primitives: a newsvendor whose stockout cost is churned lifetime value, a geometric retry multiplier in which the recycled product is dissatisfaction, and a two-regime transient queue whose arrival rate is made endogenous by retries. Statically, there is a nonempty, measurable regime in which a cheaper model saves energy per satisfied answer while consuming strictly more capacity per satisfied answer, so the discount inverts exactly when capacity binds. Dynamically, a reactive throttle fired during a surge can cross an ignition threshold beyond which it manufactures more traffic than it sheds, and a release rule set below the degraded equilibrium converts a transient surge into a permanent degraded regime. With heterogeneous customers, throttling is a transportation problem in retry-inflated load whose optimal policy rations intelligence by critical ratio, class by class, and whose dual, the shadow price of intelligence, prices a marginal query by class and by hour; closed-form trajectories make it computable in milliseconds. Stochastic analysis sharpens rather than erodes the thesis: the ignition boundary acquires a predicted width, and noise punishes the reactive policy that parks the system against it. Under congestion, throttling is not a cost lever but a demand lever.

</details>


### [72] [Existence of Relaxed Equilibrium for Time-Inconsistent Mean Field Games: A Set-Valued Fixed Point Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24016)
*Zhenhua Wang,Zhou Zhou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限时域连续时间下不一致均值场博弈中松弛均衡的存在性，通过在松弛反馈策略与群体流的乘积空间上构建方法，结合Young测度的稳定拓扑与Wasserstein连续流的紧凸集，利用Kakutani-Fan-Glicksberg不动点定理证明了松弛均衡的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对时间不一致性带来的动态非协调问题，传统纳什均衡难以直接应用，本文旨在建立适用于此类连续时间均值场博弈的松弛均衡存在性理论框架。

Method: 采用Young测度赋予策略空间稳定拓扑，限制群体流于具有正则性约束的紧凸Wasserstein连续流集合；对每个策略-流对建立辅助价值函数的一致Sobolev与Hölder估计，并证明其在Young测度收敛和Wasserstein收敛下的稳定性；通过Fokker-Planck方程的对偶论证证明群体流映射的连续性，构造具有非空紧凸值的集值最佳响应/一致性映射并应用不动点定理。

Result: 建立了辅助价值函数在策略与群体流联合收敛下的稳定性；证明了群体流映射的连续性；构造出满足非空、紧、凸性质的集值映射，并成功应用Kakutani-Fan-Glicksberg不动点定理得到固定点。

Conclusion: 该不动点同时满足个体博弈中的均衡响应条件与均值场一致性条件，从而证明了所研究的不一致均值场博弈中存在松弛均衡。

Abstract: This paper studies the existence of relaxed equilibria for finite-horizon continuous-time time-inconsistent mean field games. We work directly on the product space of relaxed feedback policies and population flows. The policy component is endowed with the stable topology of Young measures, while the population component is restricted to a compact convex set of Wasserstein-continuous flows with uniform moment and time-regularity bounds. For every policy-flow pair, we establish uniform Sobolev and Hölder estimates for the associated auxiliary value function and prove its stability under Young-measure convergence of policies and uniform Wasserstein convergence of population flows. We also establish continuity of the induced population-flow map. The latter requires a duality argument for the Fokker-Planck equations because Young-measure convergence yields only weak-$*$ convergence of the controlled drifts. We then construct a set-valued best-response/consistency map with nonempty compact convex values and apply the Kakutani-Fan-Glicksberg fixed-point theorem. The resulting fixed point satisfies both the equilibrium response condition of the intra-personal game and the mean field consistency condition, , thereby establishing the existence of a relaxed equilibrium

</details>


### [73] [Safe Distributed Generalized Nash Equilibrium Seeking via Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24077)
*Yihan Meng,Weijian Li,Lacra Pavel*

TL;DR: 本文研究了在具有耦合约束集的非合作博弈中，如何通过引入控制屏障函数（CBF）来保证分布式广义纳什均衡（GNE）求解过程中的安全性，并设计了全信息与部分信息下的动力学方法，证明了约束集的正向不变性、均衡点的正确性与渐近稳定性，同时扩展至多积分器系统，通过数值仿真验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非合作博弈的分布式GNE求解中，确保系统的安全性是一个关键挑战，尤其是在存在耦合约束的情况下，传统的GNE求解方法难以有效处理动态过程中的安全约束。

Method: 引入控制屏障函数（CBF）到GNE求解动力学设计中，分别针对全信息和部分信息设置设计了相应的动力学算法，并将其扩展至多积分器动力学系统。

Conclusion: 通过引入CBF，能够在分布式GNE求解过程中有效编码安全约束，所提出的方法在理论和仿真上均能保证安全性和收敛性，适用于多种信息结构和动力学模型。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider generalized Nash equilibrium (GNE) seeking in non-cooperative games with coupled constraint sets. Specifically, we aim to enforce safety for distributed GNE seeking, whereby the safety specifications are encoded in the coupled constraint set. To achieve this, we introduce the control barrier function (CBF) in the design of the GNE seeking dynamics. We design the dynamics for both full- and partial-information setting, where each player has knowledge of the decision information of all other players or only neighboring players, respectively. We justify the proposed dynamics by showing that the coupled constraint set is forward invariant, the equilibrium of the dynamics coincides with the exact GNE of the game, and the dynamics is asymptotically stable. Furthermore, we extend the approach to games where the agents are multi-integrators. Numerical simulations are provided to verify our results.

</details>


### [74] [Quadratic Optimization over Probability Measures with Coupling Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24184)
*Hoang Anh Tran,Yong Sheng Soh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类目标函数为二次型、决策变量为概率测度的优化问题，这类问题源于最优传输（如Gromov-Wasserstein问题）和能量景观最小化等应用。由于目标函数关于测度是二次的，传统广义矩问题框架不再适用。为此，作者提出一个基于在基空间乘积上定义概率测度的凸松弛层次结构，并引入矩阵正定性的推广概念。在满足边际约束的条件下，证明了该层次结构收敛到全局最优解；当目标为多项式时，进一步构造了一个有限维半定规划的SOS型层次，其解收敛于原问题的最优值。通过数值实验验证了方法的有效性，为将SOS思想扩展至更广泛的非线性测度优化问题提供了路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受最优传输（如Gromov-Wasserstein问题）和能量景观最小化等应用驱动，需要解决目标为测度上二次函数的优化问题，而传统广义矩问题要求目标线性，无法处理此类非线性情形。

Method: 提出一种新的凸松弛层次结构，通过在基空间的乘积上搜索概率测度来逼近原问题；引入适用于测度情形的广义正半定性概念；在具有边际约束的前提下，构建可计算的有限维半定规划SOS层次以逼近最优解。

Result: 证明了所提层次结构在一般情况下收敛到全局最优解；当目标为多项式时，构造的SOS型半定规划层次也能收敛到原问题的最优值；并通过数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作成功地将SOS层次方法推广至测度空间上的二次优化问题，为处理非线性依赖于测度的优化问题提供了系统性框架，拓展了传统矩方法的应用边界。

Abstract: We consider solving an optimization instance in which the objective is quadratic and where the decision variable is a probability measure. Our class of problems are motivated by applications arising from optimal transport (with the Gromov-Wasserstein problem being a prominent example) as well as energy landscape minimization. Because the objective depends quadratically on the decision variable, our class of problems fall outside the standard modeling framework of the Generalized Moment Problems (which requires the objective to be linear). To this end, we propose a hierarchy of convex relaxations based on searching over probability measures over products of the base space. These have a natural interpretation with the moment Sum-of-squares hierarchy-a prominent framework for solving polynomial optimization instances, which we adapt to accommodate probability measures. A key conceptual contribution is to introduce a notion of positive-semidefiniteness that extends the usual notion over matrices. Under the assumption that the decision variables satisfy certain marginal constraints (as in the Kantorovich formulation of the optimal transport problem), we establish convergence of our hierarchy towards the globally optimal solution. Under the additional assumption that the objective is a polynomial, we propose a moment-SOS type hierarchy of finite dimensional semidefinite programs whose optimal solution converges to that of the original quadratic optimization over measures. We demonstrate our framework with numerical experiments. More generally, optimization over measures where the objective and/or constraint depends on the decision in a polynomial way is a fundamental problem. It is hoped that our work provides a road-map as to how the ideas of the SOS-ordinarily developed for polynomial optimization-may be applied to a broader class of non-linear problems involving measures.

</details>


### [75] [A Line-Search-Free Coordinate Proximal Predictor-Corrector Method for Monotone Absolute Value Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24346)

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在残差单调性条件下的绝对值方程（AVE）Ax-|x|=b，提出了一种基于对称部分特征的坐标邻近预测-校正（CPPC）方法，并证明了其收敛性和误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绝对值方程在优化和工程中有广泛应用，但其非光滑性和非线性带来求解挑战，尤其是在系数矩阵不对称的情况下。本文旨在通过引入残差单调性条件，拓展可解情形并设计高效算法。

Method: 提出一种AVE特定的前向-邻近分解框架，采用最大邻近残差坐标的精确标量邻近更新生成预测点，并利用正对齐恒等式实现无需回溯的全残差分离超平面校正；每步迭代仅需一次新的矩阵-向量乘法。

Result: 证明了算法的Fejér单调性、整体序列收敛性以及O(K^{-1/2})的最佳迭代残差界；当存在正的单调性裕度时，保证对任意右端项均有唯一解且全局线性收敛；数值实验表明在某些情况下迭代成本降低，求解时间更短。

Conclusion: 该CPPC方法在残差单调性条件下有效求解AVE问题，即使在非对称情形下也具备良好理论性质和计算效率，为AVE的求解提供了新的可行路径。

Abstract: We consider the absolute value equation (AVE) $Ax-|x|=b$ under residual monotonicity. We characterize this property through the symmetric part of the coefficient matrix, thereby allowing nonsymmetry, and propose a coordinate proximal predictor-corrector (CPPC) method based on an AVE-specific forward-proximal decomposition. An exact scalar proximal update on the largest proximal-residual coordinate generates a predictor point, while a positive-alignment identity certifies the ensuing full-residual separating-hyperplane correction without backtracking. With the current matrix product cached, each iteration requires one new full matrix-vector product. For a nonempty solution set, we prove Fejér monotonicity, whole-sequence convergence, and an $O(K^{-1/2})$ best-iterate residual bound. A positive monotonicity margin further ensures unique solvability for every right-hand side and global linear convergence. Numerical results identify regimes in which the reduced per-iteration work yields shorter solution times.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统盈利能力指标（如单产品毛利率）假设各生产线可独立运行，但在存在共用原料的耦合生产系统中（如聚合物工厂），该假设失效，导致次优甚至价值破坏的生产计划。因此需要一种更符合实际生产约束的评估指标。

Method: 提出了基于‘共生产列’概念和规划问题的流体松弛方法构建的AGPPC框架，并结合双线性混合整数规划模型及McCormick线性化技术提供优化边界，实现对耦合生产系统的精确建模与求解。

Result: 在印度尼西亚一家聚合物生产商的实际数据上验证了该方法的有效性：仅使用耦合感知指标即可使运营利润提升7.6%，而完整优化方案则带来28%的利润增长。

Conclusion: AGPPC框架能够有效应对耦合生产环境下的产品优先级决策挑战，相较传统方法显著提升经济效益，具备在复杂化工生产中推广应用的潜力。

Abstract: Profitability metrics, such as the single-product gross profit margin, constitute the standard toolbox to prioritize products in sales and operations planning (S&OP). These metrics leverage the premise that production lines can be committed independently, which endows them with a simple per-ton ranking that a planner can compute and use easily in practice. However, coupled plants, especially polymer plants whose parallel lines draw simultaneously on a single bulk feed, can fundamentally undermine this premise and mislead to value-destroying production plans. We propose a framework built on what we call the Augmented Gross Profit for Product Clusters (AGPPC), which converts the per-ton ranking metric into a per-coupled-hour ranking that carries a per-instance optimality certificate. We present the theory of AGPPC, which combines the co-production column concept with a fluid relaxation of the planning problem, alongside an integrated bilinear mixed integer program of the plant and a McCormick-linearized baseline that provides certified bounds for it, and we demonstrate its effectiveness on real operational data from an Indonesian polymer producer, where the coupling-aware metric alone raises operating profit by 7.6% and the full optimization by 28% over current practice.

</details>

*Saeed Sadeghi Arjmand*

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个确定性的首阶平均场博弈模型，用于描述人力资本积累过程中的教育投资行为，重点分析了个体在知识溢出和社交互动影响下的最优学习策略及其对群体分布演化的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在考虑社会交互和知识外溢的情况下，个体如何通过教育投资以最短时间达到目标资格水平，并解决学习过程中的资金约束问题。

Method: 将个体问题建模为具有时变动态和状态约束的最短时间最优控制问题，利用动态规划原理和Hamilton-Jacobi方程分析价值函数，并通过Schauder不动点定理证明均衡的存在性。

Result: 建立了最优控制与轨迹的存在性，刻画了最优学习努力水平，并推导出描述人口分布演化的连续性方程，证明了在任意有限人口时间范围内至少存在一个均衡。

Conclusion: 该模型成功整合了个体最优决策与群体动态演化，在无限个体时间尺度与有限群体时间尺度之间建立了联系，验证了平均场博弈中均衡解的存在性。

Abstract: We study a deterministic first-order mean field game model for human capital accumulation in which a continuum of agents invest in education and training to reach a certain qualification level in minimum time. The productivity of learning depends on both the individual's current level of human capital and the distribution of the population, modeling knowledge spillovers and social interactions. Learning is financed by the income generated by existing human capital, leading to a state constraint requiring the financial resources to remain nonnegative throughout the evolution. For a fixed population flow, we formulate the individual problem as a state-constrained minimum-time optimal control problem with time-dependent dynamics. We establish the existence of optimal controls and trajectories, analyze the associated value function through the Dynamic Programming Principle and a Hamilton--Jacobi equation, and characterize the optimal learning effort under suitable regularity and convexity assumptions. The optimal feedback induces a continuity equation describing the evolution of the population distribution. The resulting mean field game is formulated as a fixed-point problem for the population flow. By constructing a compact invariant set and applying Schauder's fixed-point theorem, we prove the existence of at least one equilibrium on every finite population horizon, while the individual minimum-time problem remains formulated on the infinite time horizon.

</details>

Abstract: This paper describes the electricity markets and operational processes in the Irish power system and translates them into a Mixed Integer Linear Programming (MILP) model. The model is designed to estimate renewable generation Curtailment and Constraint. A full mathematical formulation is presented and tested on both a simple example and a realistic model of the Irish transmission network. The results show that the proposed formulation works as intended, capturing curtailment and constraint effects, while also highlighting model limitations and directions for future improvement.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决带有复杂几何约束（尤其是非凸约束）的优化问题，现有方法通常依赖较强的约束规范条件，限制了其适用性，因此需要一种仅依赖较弱正则性条件的新方法。

Method: 构建了约束消解映射的一般框架，并结合不精确罚函数法设计了新算法，利用渐近Mordukhovich正则性分析收敛性。

Result: 证明了算法的收敛性，且每个可行聚点均为Mordukhovich平稳点；在等式、互补性、稀疏性和低秩约束问题上的实验表明，该方法在解的质量上与增广拉格朗日法相当，且显著优于罚分解法。

Conclusion: 所提方法在较弱正则性假设下仍具良好收敛性，扩展了约束消解方法的适用范围，在多种经典约束问题上表现出强竞争力。

Abstract: Optimization problems with geometric constraints have a broad range of applications, including machine learning, finance, and control. A powerful algorithmic tool to resolve these geometric constraints are constraint dissolving methods. To this end, we propose a framework for constraint dissolving mappings for nonconvex geometric constraints. Leveraging these, we develop a constraint dissolving inexact penalty method to solve optimization problems with general set-membership constraints and possibly nonconvex geometric constraints. We establish the convergence of the proposed algorithm and prove that every feasible accumulation point is Mordukhovich stationary. Notably, we rely only on mild asymptotic Mordukhovich regularity, which is significantly weaker than the constraint qualifications adopted in the existing literature on constraint dissolving methods. Numerical experiments addressing classical equality-, complementarity-, sparsity-, and low-rank constrained optimization problems demonstrate that the proposed method is competitive with the safeguarded augmented Lagrangian method in terms of solution quality and significantly outperforms the penalty decomposition method.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的嵌入维度选择多依赖启发式方法，缺乏理论依据，且忽视了不同类别变量在近似能力和参数成本上的异质性，导致模型复杂度与泛化能力难以平衡。

Method: 将嵌入容量分配建模为在全局嵌入预算下的约束优化问题，通过奇异值尾部刻画近似误差，以总嵌入复杂度衡量估计误差，推导出每个预测变量的最优维度与其近似价值平方根成正比、与参数成本成反比的闭式分配规则。

Result: 模拟实验验证了所提近似-估计权衡的有效性，在预算紧张且预测变量异质性强时，新方法相比均匀分配和基于基数的启发式方法显著提高了预算效率；在真实医疗数据中进一步提升了预测准确性和概率校准。

Conclusion: 嵌入维度分配可被建模为有原则的有限样本优化问题，而非仅依赖启发式调参，为高维异质分类变量的表示学习提供了理论支持与实用工具。

Abstract: The embedding dimension of categorical predictors is usually selected through heuristic tuning, although it directly affects model complexity, approximation quality, and finite-sample generalization. This paper formulates embedding dimension selection as a constrained allocation problem. The main contribution is to show that embedding capacity can be allocated across heterogeneous categorical predictors according to an explicit approximation-estimation tradeoff. We characterize approximation error through the singular-value tail of the latent category representation, while estimation error increases with total embedding complexity. Under a fixed global embedding budget and a tractable approximation model, this leads to a closed-form allocation rule in which the dimension assigned to each predictor is proportional to the square root of its approximation value relative to its parameter cost. Simulation experiments support the proposed approximation-estimation interpretation and show that the allocation rule improves budget efficiency relative to standard uniform and cardinality-based heuristics, particularly when the budget is binding and predictor heterogeneity is substantial. A real-data healthcare application further shows improvements in predictive accuracy and probabilistic calibration. Overall, the results establish embedding-dimension allocation as a principled finite-sample optimization problem rather than a purely heuristic modeling choice.

</details>

Abstract: Linear programming (LP) formulations offer a conceptually elegant approach to infinite-horizon, model-free nonlinear optimal control in continuous spaces. However, in addition to the curse of dimensionality, their practical use is limited by the difficulty of consistently obtaining bounded solutions. In this work, we use moment-matching techniques to derive sufficient boundedness conditions in terms of the available dataset and the cost vector of the LP. Moreover, we discuss practical design methods for nonlinear systems and polynomial features.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统梯度下降方法在处理非光滑或复杂优化问题时收敛速度慢且易陷入振荡，缺乏对历史搜索信息的有效利用，因此需要引入具有记忆特性的分数阶导数并设计自适应机制以提升优化性能。

Method: 提出Caputo分数阶加速梯度下降法（CFAGD），引入自适应(\b{eta}^k)参数调整搜索方向，结合Caputo分数阶导数保留历史信息，并采用Armijo型非精确线搜索确定步长，通过正参数缩放步长以生成下降序列。

Result: 在强凸性和有界Hessian假设下证明了该方法的线性收敛性；数值实验包括基于神经网络的例子，结果表明CFAGD在收敛速度和稳定性方面优于对比方法。

Conclusion: CFAGD通过融合分数阶微积分的记忆特性与自适应参数调节机制，有效提升了优化过程的效率与鲁棒性，为无约束优化问题提供了新的高性能求解工具。

Abstract: This article proposes a Caputo fractional accelerated gradient descent (CFAGD) method for unconstrained optimization problems that is applicable to both smooth and a class of non-smooth objective functions. The proposed approach incorporates an adaptive (\b{eta}k)-parameter, which is heuristically updated throughout the iterative process to improve the search direction. Furthermore, the method employs the Caputo fractional derivative together with the adaptive (\b{eta}^k)-parameter, thereby preserving the memory characteristics associated with non-integer-order derivatives. A suitable step-size is selected via an inexact line-search technique based on the Armijo condition. The central idea is to scale the step-size by a positive parameter to improve the behavior of the iterates as they approach an optimal point, thereby generating a descent sequence. Under strong convexity and bounded Hessian assumptions, linear convergence of the proposed method is established. Numerical validations, including neural-network-based examples, further indicate that the CFAGD method can achieve faster and more stable performance than competing approaches.

</details>

### [83] [Predictability of El Niño from Delayed Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24428)

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: 延迟观测显著提升了Niño-3.4指数的预测能力，但增加模型复杂性并未带来系统性改进，表明过去信息的表征比模型复杂性更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究延迟观测中包含多少可用于预测ENSO的有用信息，并评估不同模型复杂度对预测技能的贡献。

Method: 使用岭回归识别有信息量的延迟项，并比较多种线性和非线性模型（包括多层感知机、SINDy、GRU和LSTM）的预测性能。

Result: 延迟观测在长达六个月的预测中显著优于持续性预测和气候学预测；然而，增加模型复杂性并未系统提升预测技能；历史递归实验更倾向于简单的显式SINDy递推模型和浅层循环结构。

Conclusion: Niño-3.4演变存在紧凑的可预测表征，过去信息的表示方式比模型复杂性更为关键。

Abstract: Using monthly Niño-3.4 anomalies through July 2026, we investigate how much predictive information is contained in delayed observations of the index. Ridge regression identifies informative delays, while multilayer perceptron and sparse identification of nonlinear dynamics (SINDy) models test whether nonlinear complexity provides additional direct forecast skill; gated recurrent unit (GRU) and long short-term memory (LSTM) networks provide a complementary test in which the temporal representation is learned internally. Delayed observations substantially improve forecasts over persistence and climatology at leads of up to six months, but increasing model complexity provides no systematic improvement. Historical recursive experiments favor a simple explicit SINDy recurrence and select shallow recurrent architectures, with no appreciable gain from learning the temporal representation internally. These results support a compact predictive representation of Niño-3.4 evolution in which the representation of past information is more consequential than model complexity. As a prospective application, the selected models are used to forecast the developing 2026 event beyond the last available observation and to compare its predicted evolution with completed historical El Niño events.

</details>


### [84] [AICON: An operational global machine learning weather forecasting model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24651)
*Tobias Goecke,Marek Jacob,Florian Prill,Michael Denhard,Felix Fundel,Jan Keller,Roland Potthast,Hendrik Reich,Britta Seegebrecht,Sven Ulbrich,Arianna Valmassoi,Sabrina Wahl*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: AICON是一个基于图神经网络的全球机器学习天气预报模型，使用高分辨率ICON-DREAM数据集训练，空间分辨率达13公里，时间步长为3小时，已在德国气象局投入业务运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在提升中小尺度天气特征的预报精度，避免传统模型在长期预测优化中对小尺度特征的过度平滑问题。

Method: 采用基于二十面体多网格的图神经网络架构，结合编码器-处理器-解码器结构和图注意力机制，直接利用ICON模型的原生网格结构，并使用SLEVE垂直坐标系；训练中避免自回归多步展开，专注于短期高保真模拟。

Result: 在短中期预报中对近地面变量的表现与业务化的ICON模型相当甚至更优，谱分析和热带气旋案例显示其在多尺度大气变率表征上的优势与局限。

Conclusion: AICON通过与物理模型一致的网格结构和专注小尺度保真的训练策略，成为高性能的机器学习天气预报模型，已成功投入实际业务应用。

Abstract: We introduce AICON, a global machine learning weather prediction (MLWP) model which generates forecasts at 13 km spatial resolution with a 3-hour time step, trained on the high-resolution, non-hydrostatic ICON-DREAM dataset. AICON is in full operational use at Deutscher Wetterdienst since 2nd of March 2026. The model employs a graph neural network (GNN) architecture with an encoder-processor-decoder structure, where node updates are performed using a graph attention mechanism. A key feature of AICON is its use of an icosahedral multi-mesh derived from the native grid of the ICON model, ensuring consistency with the training data. ICON's terrain-following vertical SLEVE coordinate is one of the major distinctions from existing emulators. AICON's training strategy prioritizes small-scale fidelity by avoiding autoregressive multi-step rollout and longer forecast horizons during training, a design choice motivated by the hypothesis that this approach preserves fine-scale features often damped in models optimized for longer-range forecasts. We describe the prognostic and diagnostic variables used for training, the transfer learning protocol employed to accelerate convergence, and the model's performance across a range of evaluation metrics. An extensive evaluation, including routine verification against observation, a tropical cyclone case and spectral analysis reveal the strengths and limitations in the representation of atmospheric variability across scales. Routine verification against observations demonstrates competitive skill relative to the operational ICON model, particularly for near-surface variables in the short to medium forecast range.

</details>

### [85] [Ranking by points and ordinal models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23859)
*Leszek Szczecinski*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了体育联赛中积分规则的设计，发现当且仅当比赛为常数和（即每场比赛在参与者之间分配固定数量的积分）且结果服从以积分为斜率参数的相邻类别模型时，总积分才是数据的充分统计量。现行一些联赛（如足球、冰球）的积分规则不满足该条件，导致排名与基于技能估计的排序不一致。文章指出，在赛程平衡的情况下，积分排名与技能估计一致，而当前许多联赛的规则不如统一每结果一分的规则更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的体育联赛积分系统通常人为设定胜负平对应的积分，缺乏统计学基础。作者希望从充分统计量的角度出发，探讨什么样的积分规则能使总积分真正反映选手或球队的真实能力。

Method: 通过建立概率模型（相邻类别模型），分析积分作为充分统计量的条件，并比较不同积分规则下排名与基于模型估计的能力排序的一致性，尤其是在赛程安排对称情况下的表现。

Result: 1. 积分是充分统计量当且仅当积分规则为常数和且符合相邻类别模型；2. 常见联赛如NHL和足球（胜场3分）不满足此条件；3. 在对手和主客场对称的循环赛中，积分排名与能力估计一致；4. 多个真实联赛数据分析显示，统一每结果一分的规则比现行规则更接近能力估计排序。

Conclusion: 当前许多体育联赛的积分规则在统计意义上并非最优，建议采用更符合统计原理的常数和或统一积分规则，以使排名更好反映真实技能水平。

Abstract: Sport leagues assign a fixed score-point to each match outcome and rank the players (or teams) by their accumulated scores. Since the score-points are mandated without a probabilistic model in mind, we investigate models that make the score the right summary of the data. We find the score to be a sufficient statistic if and only if the points rule is constant-sum, so that every match distributes a fixed number of points between its participants, and the outcomes follow the adjacent categories model whose slope parameters are the score-points themselves. The constant-sum condition fails in the National Hockey League and in football, which awards three points for a win instead of the two required by sufficiency. We also establish that when two players meet every other opponent equally often, and each plays as often at home as away, they are ordered identically by the score and by the estimated skills; a venue-balanced round-robin satisfies the condition for all pairs. The Elo ranking then produces the same order. In nine leagues from football, ice hockey and volleyball, the two orders differ only where the schedule condition fails, and the uniform rule awarding one point per outcome level agrees with the estimated skills more closely than each league's own rule, unless that rule is already the uniform one.

</details>


### [86] [Learning from Uncertainty-dependent Missing Labels for Semi-supervised Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23960)
*You-Gan Wang,Jinran Wu,Geoffrey J. McLachlan*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了在半监督学习中，标签缺失机制依赖于分类后验不确定性时的信息理论框架，提出了基于似然的信息分解方法，表明在这种机制下缺失指示符可作为增强信息的信号，能提升估计与分类效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于标签缺失通常被视为信息损失，在特定机制下（如依赖分类不确定性的缺失），如何利用缺失模式本身提供额外信息成为关键问题。

Method: 采用基于似然的信息理论，推导出Fisher信息分解，并在模型正确设定与联合误设情况下分别分析敏感性与协方差结构，结合插件分类器与边际风险界进行分析。

Result: 得出缺失机制曲率项为非负，证明有利的缺失机制可增加信息量；在高斯混合等设定下实现n^{-1}的参数收敛速率，且常数由判别方向上的调整信息决定。

Conclusion: 当标签缺失依赖于分类不确定性时，缺失模式本身可作为信息源，在固定标注预算下提升学习效率，且信息不会超过同时观测标签与机制指标的增广实验上限。

Abstract: Missing labels are usually regarded as a source of information loss in classification. We study a semi-supervised setting in which the probability of label missingness depends on the observed features through posterior classification uncertainty. In this setting, the missingness indicator is not only a record of an unobserved label, but also an observable signal generated by a mechanism linked to the classifier. We develop a likelihood-based information theory for such uncertainty-dependent missing labels. Under correct specification, we derive a Fisher-information decomposition that separates a partial-labeling component from a nonnegative mechanism-curvature term. Under joint misspecification of the label model and the missingness mechanism, we obtain the corresponding Godambe--Eicker--Huber--White sensitivity and sandwich-covariance partitions. We also clarify the relevant complete-data benchmark: favorable missingness can increase information relative to ordinary fully labeled or budget-matched non-informative labeling baselines, but cannot exceed the information in the augmented experiment in which labels and mechanism indicators are both observed. For plug-in classifiers, we connect the information decomposition to margin-based excess-risk bounds. In regular two-component mixture settings this yields the parametric \(n^{-1}\) excess-risk rate, with constants determined by the nuisance-adjusted information in discriminant directions. Gaussian-mixture calculations and a medical diagnosis example illustrate how uncertainty-dependent labeling mechanisms can improve estimation and classification under a fixed labeling budget.

</details>


### [87] [Testing for Stable Intervals in Non-Stationary Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24194)
*Florian Heinrichs*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种非平稳回归模型中稳定区间的存在性检验方法，适用于具有依赖性和局部平稳误差的时间序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多时间序列在整个观测期间并不稳定，但可能存在科学上有意义的相对稳定的阶段。为了检测这些在给定容忍度内保持稳定的时段，需要一种有效的统计检验方法。

Method: 通过局部线性回归估计信号d，并构建基于d_∞的插件检验；利用极值集概念形式化极值窗口的局部性，推导核估计量在可能收缩索引集上的高斯和极值分布近似。

Result: 所提检验具有一致性且能控制渐近水平；理论可推广至时变长期方差和基于导数的相关变化检验。模拟和实际生理及工程时间序列应用验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法为非平稳时间序列中识别显著稳定期提供了可靠的统计工具，具有良好的理论性质和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Many time series are not stable over their full observation horizon, but may contain scientifically meaningful periods during which a signal remains stable up to a prescribed tolerance. We formulate this as an existence test for stable intervals in a non-stationary regression model with dependent, locally stationary errors. For a signal $d$ derived from the mean function, including deviations from reference levels and derivatives, stability over duration $δ$ is encoded by $d_\infty=\inf_{t\in[0,1-δ]}\sup_{s\in[t,t+δ]}|d(s)|$. The hypothesis $d_\infty\geΔ$ states that no interval of length $δ$ remains within the tolerance $Δ$, while rejection provides evidence for the existence of a relevant stable period. We estimate $d$ by local linear regression and construct plug-in tests for $d_\infty$. The asymptotic distribution is determined only by near-extremal windows at which the minimax functional is attained. We formalize this localization through extremal sets and derive Gaussian and extreme value approximations for kernel estimators over possibly shrinking index sets with time-varying long-run variance. The resulting tests are consistent and have asymptotic level control. The theory also extends supremum-based relevant-change tests to time-varying long-run variance and derivative-based hypotheses. Simulations and applications to physiological and engineering time series illustrate the method.

</details>


### [88] [Maximum effective dimension and information gain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24450)
*David Janz,Arya Akhavan,Alexandre B. Tsybakov*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文建立了核Gram矩阵在任意设计下的最大有效维度和信息增益的通用上界，并表明在三种正则性机制下这些上界无法被进一步改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了理解核方法在不同设计下的性能极限，研究核Gram矩阵的有效维度和信息增益的上界。

Method: 通过分析核的逼近性质或相应的再生核希尔伯特空间来推导上界，并在三种正则性机制下验证其紧致性。

Result: 得出了适用于多种核（如Matérn和平方指数核）的通用上界，并证明这些上界在常数因子内是最优的。

Conclusion: 所提出的上界在广泛条件下是紧致的，为核方法的理论分析提供了重要支撑。

Abstract: We establish general upper bounds on the maximum effective dimension and information gain of kernel Gram matrices over arbitrary designs in terms of approximation properties of the kernel or, equivalently, of the corresponding reproducing kernel Hilbert space. We show that across three regularity regimes, covering as special cases the Matérn and squared exponential kernels, these bounds cannot be improved beyond constant factors.

</details>


### [89] [On the privacy cost for dependent Gaussian data: spectral density estimation under local differential privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24847)
*Yann Issartel,François Roueff*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了在局部差分隐私（LDP）下估计中心平稳高斯过程依赖结构的基本问题，通过建立极小极大下界和匹配的上界，解决了先前存在的α²与α⁴之间的差距，并揭示了时间依赖性带来的额外隐私代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在局部差分隐私下谱密度估计中存在的极小极大率中α²与α⁴之间的开放性差距，并理解时间依赖性对隐私成本的影响。

Method: 利用私有化依赖高斯观测的收缩边界证明极小极大下界，并设计特定于问题的算法以达到该下界所确定的速率，而非应用通用的隐私机制到经典估计量。

Result: 建立了Sobolev型谱密度类上的极小极大下界，显示高隐私区域的有效样本量为Nα⁴；提出了消除先前工作中对数损失的匹配上界；并将工具应用于相关问题，如填补固定滞后自协方差估计中的对数间隙、证明α⁴代价在每个远离零的谱密度附近局部存在，以及表明在LDP下经典的渐近等价性通常不成立。

Conclusion: 时间依赖性而非边缘分布是导致LDP下额外隐私代价（即Nα⁴效应）的根本原因，且本文提出的方法能够紧致地刻画谱密度估计及其他相关问题的极小极大性能极限。

Abstract: We study the fundamental problem of estimating the dependence structure of a centered stationary Gaussian process under local differential privacy (LDP). In this setting, the spectral density characterizes the dependence structure of the data and is the quantity to be estimated. Our main contribution is to close the open $α^2$-versus-$α^4$ gap between the previously known lower and upper bounds on the minimax rate. Specifically, we establish a minimax lower bound showing that, over Sobolev-type classes of spectral densities, the effective sample size in the high-privacy regime is $Nα^4$, rather than the usual $Nα^2$ arising for independent observations. This additional privacy cost is caused by the temporal dependence between the observations rather than by their marginal distributions. The proof relies on a contraction bound for privatized dependent Gaussian observations. Our second contribution is a matching upper bound, free of the polylogarithmic losses present in previous work. Rather than applying a generic privatization scheme to classical estimators, we construct a problem-specific procedure attaining the rate identified by our lower bound. Beyond closing the gaps in spectral density estimation, we apply the tools developed for this problem to several related questions. We (i) close the logarithmic gap for fixed-lag autocovariance estimation, (ii) show that the $α^4$ cost arises locally around every spectral density bounded away from zero, and (iii) establish that classical asymptotic equivalence with an independent Gaussian experiment generally fails under LDP.

</details>

*Quoc Thai Tran,Duc P. Truong,William Dai,Kim Ø. Rasmussen*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于张量列车（TT）格式的高效方法，用于求解由等几何分析离散的大规模三维线性弹性问题，通过利用基函数和系数场的低秩结构实现显著压缩和可扩展计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了应对大规模三维线性弹性问题在传统稀疏全网格表示下面临的高计算成本和存储需求，本文旨在开发一种更高效、可扩展的求解方法。

Method: 利用基函数的张量积结构和几何依赖系数场的低秩特性，将刚度算子、质量算子、力向量和位移解表示为TT格式；研究了块算子和单算子两种TT形式，并分别采用矩阵自由的三重场TT共轭梯度法和AMEn算法进行求解。

Result: 数值实验表明，与传统的稀疏全网格表示相比，TT格式在算子和解上均实现了显著的数据压缩，且计算具有良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 基于TT的张量分解方法为大规模三维弹性模拟提供了一种高效且可扩展的解决方案，有效降低了存储和计算负担。

Abstract: This work develops tensor-train (TT) formulations for solving large-scale three-dimensional linear elasticity problems discretized by isogeometric analysis. By exploiting the tensor-product structure of the basis functions and the low-rank structure of geometry-dependent coefficient fields, the stiffness operator, mass operator, force vector, and displacement solution are represented in TT format. Two solution strategies are investigated: a block-operator formulation, in which the coupled elasticity operator is stored as separated TT blocks, and a single-operator formulation, in which the full coupled system is stored as one monolithic TT operator. A matrix-free three-field TT conjugate-gradient solver is introduced for the block formulation, while AMEn is used for the single-operator formulation. Numerical examples demonstrate substantial compression of both operators and solutions compared with conventional sparse full-grid representations, showing that TT-based formulations provide an efficient and scalable approach for large-scale three-dimensional elasticity simulations.

</details>


### [91] [Algorithmic Cost in "Exact Real Computation"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23603)
*Jihoon Hyun,Holger Thies,Martin Ziegler*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了精确实数计算（ERC）的多项式时间等价性，通过为ERC的操作原语分配比特成本，证明了其与图灵机在多项式时间内可计算函数上的等价性，并通过iRRAM C++库的实现验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升精确实数计算（ERC）这一数值编程语言范式的实用性，需要从定性等价扩展到定量的多项式时间等价，以更好地反映其计算效率。

Method: 通过为ERC的操作原语（包括部分/多值测试）分配比特成本，建立其与图灵机在多项式时间内的对应关系，并利用iRRAM C++库进行运行时测量以验证理论结果。

Result: 证明了在ERC中具有多项式成本的实函数可在图灵机上以多项式时间计算，反之亦然；实验结果支持理论分析。

Conclusion: ERC不仅在定性上等价于图灵机，在定量上也实现了多项式时间等价，增强了其作为可计算分析中实用编程模型的地位。

Abstract: Turing completeness of a programming language or system characterizes its expressive power; and the strong Church-Turing hypo-/thesis refines such from qualitative to polynomial-time equivalence. Exact Real Computation (ERC) is a novel numerical programming language paradigm: for the imperative processing of continuous data as entities appearing as exact, i.e. devoid of rounding errors [doi:10.1007/978-3-662-44199-2_107]. ERC has been designed [doi:10.46298/lmcs-20(2:17)2024] as convenient and practical alternative, namely proven qualitatively equivalent, to the Turing machines originally underlying Computable Analysis [doi:10.1007/978-3-642-56999-9,doi:10.1007/978-1-4684-6802-1].
  The present work quantitatively strengthens this qualitative Turing-completeness: We assign bit-costs to ERC's operational primitives (including partial/multivalued tests) in such a way that any real function incurring polynomial cost becomes Turing-computable in polynomial time, and vice versa. Runtime measurements on implementations in the iRRAM C++ library confirm our theoretical performance predictions.

</details>


### [92] [The least squares RBF-PU method for linear elasticity in the diaphragm geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23718)
*Andreas Michael,Elisabeth Larsson,Pierre-Frédéric Villard,Davoud Mirzaei*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于薄几何结构的非适配最小二乘径向基函数分区单元法，用于模拟真实膈肌在弹性模型和实际边界条件下的变形，并给出了该方法对问题高阶收敛的理论证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于膈肌几何形状非常薄，其数值模拟对大多数数值方法构成了挑战，而机械通气患者呼吸并发症的研究需要精确的膈肌变形模拟。

Method: 采用非适配最小二乘径向基函数分区单元法（unfitted least-squares radial basis function partition of unity method），利用其非适配特性及圆柱形 patches 的形状与细化能力来求解薄几何结构问题。

Result: 成功计算了真实膈肌几何结构在弹性模型和真实边界条件下的变形，并从理论上证明了该方法对该类问题具有高阶收敛性。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法非常适合于薄壁结构如膈肌的数值模拟，具备高精度和良好的收敛性能，为相关生物力学研究提供了有效工具。

Abstract: The human diaphragm is vital for respiration and its simulation can aid in further understanding complications arising in individuals that have been subjected to mechanical ventilation. The diaphragm geometry is very thin and hence its numerical simulation poses a challenge for most numerical methods. In this paper, we compute the deformation of real diaphragm geometries which are modelled as linearly elastic and are subject to realistic boundary conditions. The method used is the unfitted least-squares radial basis function partition of unity method, which is well suited to solve problems on thin geometries given its unfitted nature and the shape and refinement of the cylindrical patches that are used. We derive a theoretical proof showing that the method converges with high order to the solution of this problem.

</details>


### [93] [Computing statistical Euler limits of the Navier--Stokes equations in three dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23786)
*Johannes L. Grafen,Tobias Rohner,Stephan Simonis*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于蒙特卡洛格子玻尔兹曼方法的高效算法，用于计算三维不可压Navier-Stokes和Euler方程的统计解，在零粘性极限下实现了稳定一致的数值解，并首次实现了三维时间依赖统计解的计算，数值实验验证了K41标度律并提供了Euler方程统计解普适性的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在零粘性极限下稳定且一致地求解三维不可压Navier-Stokes和Euler方程的统计解，克服传统方法在高维不确定性下的不稳定性与收敛性问题。

Method: 开发了一种结合熵时空自适应松弛的蒙特卡洛格子玻尔兹曼方法，通过离散动力学假设，在扩散尺度下证明子序列收敛至Navier-Stokes方程的Foias-Temam Liouville形式解；进一步提升至Fjordholm-Mishra-Weber框架，在结构函数一致标度下证明零粘性极限满足多点统计Euler层次结构，并推导出约0.5阶的Wasserstein收敛率。

Result: 实现了三维时间依赖统计解在无粘极限下的首次计算；数值实验显示Kolmogorov K41标度律被成功恢复，路径强收敛失效，且符合预测的Wasserstein收敛速率；通过与谱超粘性计算对比，提供了统计解普适性的数值证据。

Conclusion: 该方法为三维不可压流体在零粘性极限下的统计解提供了稳定、收敛的数值途径，验证了统计解在湍流模拟中的合理性与普适性，为理解Euler方程的非唯一性问题提供了新工具。

Abstract: We develop a Monte Carlo lattice Boltzmann method to efficiently compute statistical solutions to the three-dimensional incompressible Navier--Stokes and Euler equations. By employing entropic space-time adaptive relaxation for higher order kinetic moments, our scheme is found to yield stable and consistent numerical solutions in the vanishing viscosity limit. We establish a convergence result that is conditional on four explicitly stated assumptions regarding the discrete dynamics. First, under diffusive scaling, the laws of the discrete ensemble are shown to converge along a subsequence to a limit satisfying the Foias--Temam Liouville formulation of the Navier--Stokes equations. Second, by lifting these measures to the Fjordholm--Mishra--Weber framework, we show that, provided the structure-function scaling holds uniformly, the vanishing viscosity limit satisfies the multi-point statistical Euler hierarchy. Unconditionally, we establish weak-strong uniqueness of the limit measures on the interval of existence of a strong Euler solution, and under explicit scaling assumptions, we formally derive a fractional 1-Wasserstein convergence rate of approximately $0.5$. Our method enables the first three-dimensional computations of time-dependent statistical solutions along the inviscid limit of the incompressible Navier--Stokes equations. Numerical experiments on a randomized Taylor--Green vortex with 24-dimensional initial uncertainty successfully recover Kolmogorov's K41 scaling for energy spectra and structure functions, exhibit the failure of pathwise strong convergence, and are consistent with the predicted Wasserstein convergence rate. Finally, based on error measurements with respect to spectral hyperviscosity computations, we provide numerical evidence for the universality of statistical solutions of the incompressible Euler equations.

</details>


### [94] [Randomized Block Davidson Eigensolvers for Plane-Wave Density-Functional Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24529)
*Moritz Gubler,Taejun Park,Augustin Bussy,Johannes Laute,Nicola Marzari,Iurii Timrov,Laura Grigori*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于随机块Davidson方法的迭代对角化算法，通过随机Gram-Schmidt正交化和草图内积减少平面波密度泛函理论中的正交化开销，虽在大矩阵问题中表现优越，但在全自洽场计算中优势受限于所需态数随系统规模的增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面波密度泛函理论中迭代对角化的正交化步骤计算代价高，尤其随问题规模和目标态数增加而迅速增长，需寻找更高效的替代方案。

Method: 采用随机块Davidson型求解器，用基于草图内积的随机Gram-Schmidt替代传统的欧几里得正交化，仅需单次遍历基底，并保持瑞利-里兹投影步的精确性与赫米特广义本征问题的结构。

Result: 在固定数量本征对的稀疏问题中，当矩阵维数超过约2×10⁴时，该方法超越确定性 counterpart，在5×10⁵时快25%；但在完整自洽场DFT计算中，相比LOBPCG仅提速5-11%，且随着所需态数随系统增大，正交化节省被广义本征问题抵消。

Conclusion: 草图方法的优势取决于所求态数随问题维度的增长方式，而非求解器本身，因此其有效范围受制于系统规模与目标态数的比例关系。

Abstract: Iterative diagonalization is the dominant cost of plane-wave density-functional theory (DFT), with search-space orthogonalization scaling particularly quickly with problem size and the number of target states. We present a randomized block Davidson-type eigensolver that replaces Euclidean orthogonalization with randomized Gram-Schmidt in a sketched inner product, requiring only a single pass over the basis while keeping its conditioning bounded independently of the input vectors. This modification changes only the Rayleigh-Ritz step, which becomes a definite generalized Hermitian eigenproblem. Ritz extraction remains exact, preserving true Ritz pairs and the interlacing property that makes each band energy an upper bound on the true one. The method is implemented in mixed precision for CPUs and GPUs from a single Julia code, interfaces matrix-free with DFTK, and is released in the open-source RandESC library. On sparse test problems with a fixed number of eigenpairs, the sketched solver overtakes its deterministic counterpart beyond matrix dimensions of about $2\times 10^4$ and is $25\%$ faster at $5\times 10^5$. In full self-consistent field DFT calculations, however, both Davidson variants outperform the locally optimal block preconditioned conjugate gradient (LOBPCG) reference only by $5$ to $11\%$ in total time, while the additional benefit of sketching is limited. As the number of requested states grows with system size, orthogonalization savings are offset by the generalized eigenproblem. Therefore, the regime in which sketching pays off is set by how the number of wanted states scales with the problem dimension, not by the eigensolver as such.

</details>


### [95] [The PWDG-UWVF Equivalence Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23822)
*Shelvean Kapita*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文解释了超弱变分形式（UWVF）与特雷夫茨间断伽辽金法（Trefftz-DG）在特定参数下的等价性，揭示其本质为同一边耦合问题在不同坐标系下的表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阐明UWVF作为PWDG格式在特定参数下的特例的结构性原因，超越传统罚函数解释。

Method: 通过分析内部网格边上的跳跃项与阻抗传输缺陷的关系，引入特雷夫茨-DG范数作为全局传输缺陷的自然Riesz范数，并进行坐标变换分析。

Result: 发现PWDG在α=β=δ=1/2时的跳越项恰好是UWVF中两个定向阻抗传输缺陷平方范数之和的一半；推导出离散算子的正规形式K+iI和1/2 I+iK_U，分别对应PWDG和UWVF。

Conclusion: 经典等价性实质上是断裂场坐标与阻抗迹坐标下同一边耦合问题的不同表述，而非仅是罚参数的特殊选择。

Abstract: The ultra-weak variational formulation (UWVF) is known to be the Trefftz discontinuous Galerkin scheme obtained from the standard PWDG flux family by setting \(α=β=δ=1/2\). We give a short structural explanation of this fact. On every interior mesh edge, the two PWDG jump terms at these parameter values are exactly one half of the sum of the squared norms of the two oriented impedance-transmission defects used by the UWVF. Thus the Trefftz-DG norm is the natural Riesz norm of the global transmission defect. In PWDG coordinates its Gramian is the anti-Hermitian part of the discrete matrix, and Riesz normalization puts the operator in the normal form \(K+\ii I\), with \(K\) Hermitian. The same argument applied directly to the discrete UWVF yields the complementary normal form \(\tfrac12 I+\ii K_{\rm U}\). This identifies the classical equivalence as a relation between broken-field and impedance-trace coordinates for the same edge-coupling problem, rather than only as a particular choice of penalties.

</details>

*Jennifer Amador Peña,Sara de Ángel,Fernanda Porras*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文通过引入向量值链和上链，构建了一个与Christiansen离散弹性复形同构的新复形，并定义了从光滑弹性复形到该离散复形的类似de Rham映射。

Method: 利用多项式空间和分布空间的自由度，构建基于单纯复形几何结构的向量值链与上链，并建立相应的代数算子及de Rham-like映射。

Result: 得到了一个与Christiansen复形同构的代数复形，其算子依赖于单纯复形的几何信息，并成功构造了从光滑复形到离散复形的映射。

Conclusion: 该工作为离散弹性理论提供了新的代数框架，有助于数值方法中弹性问题的结构保持离散化。

Abstract: In this report, we recall Christiansen's [2011, Num. Math.] discrete elasticity complex that uses spaces of polynomials and distributions. Using the degrees of freedom of those spaces, we write a complex using vector-valued chains and co-chains that is isomorphic to Christiansen's complex. The operators of the new complex are algebraic, but use the geometry of the simplicial complex. Finally, we define a de Rham-like map from the smooth elasticity complex to this discrete complex.

</details>


### [97] [New Efficient Implicit-Explicit Deferred Correction methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23919)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高DeC方法在求解ODE时的计算效率，并实现精度与离散化阶数的系统匹配，引入插值机制以减少低阶迭代的计算开销，同时增强方法在实际应用中的自适应能力。

Method: 提出一种基于插值的改进型IMEX DeC方法，在连续迭代之间引入插值过程，使每次迭代的精度与所用离散化阶数一致；并在两种DeC格式家族中进行分析，通过数值实验验证其性能。

Result: 改进后的方法在处理刚性ODE和含高阶空间导数的PDE时表现出高保真度结果和更低的计算成本，同时展现出良好的稳定性与自适应效果。

Conclusion: 所提出的插值增强型IMEX DeC方法兼具高效性与自适应性，适用于复杂问题的高精度数值模拟，具有较强的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate implicit-explicit (IMEX) arbitrary high-order Deferred Correction (DeC) methods for the approximation of ordinary differential equations (ODEs). Such schemes are characterized by an iterative procedure that increases the order of accuracy by one at each iteration. More precisely, we study an efficient modification based on the introduction of interpolation processes between consecutive iterations, with the aim of systematically matching the accuracy achieved at each iteration with the order of the discretization employed. On the one hand, this modification leads to computational advantages, since the low-order iterations are performed on cheaper lower-order discretization structures; on the other hand, it endows the methods with a natural $p$-adaptive character, which is particularly appealing in the context of practical applications. We investigate this modification for two families of DeC schemes, providing numerical validation, efficiency assessments, and stability region plots. The numerical validation includes several examples involving stiff ODEs and partial differential equations (PDEs) with high-order spatial derivatives. The ability of the modified schemes to provide high-fidelity results at reduced computational cost, as well as the effectiveness of the adaptive strategy, is demonstrated through the numerical experiments.

</details>


### [98] [Structure-preserving generalized transferable neural networks for the Cahn-Hilliard equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23980)
