<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在数值求解Cahn-Hilliard方程时同时保持质量守恒和能量稳定性，并克服传统方法在复杂域或长时间模拟中的局限性，需要发展一种新的结构保持、无需网格且高效的计算方法。

Method: 采用广义可转移神经网络（GTransNet）进行空间逼近，结合稳定化的向后微分公式（BDF）进行时间离散，通过配置法实现，并引入一种新的后处理质量守恒投影来强制满足质量守恒约束。

Result: 所提出的GTransNet-BDF格式在时间离散层面上实现了质量守恒和能量稳定性；数值实验验证了方法的收敛性、质量守恒性、能量耗散性以及在二维和三维问题中的高精度与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方法是一种高效、结构保持、无需网格的神经网络框架，适用于复杂几何、变迁移率和长时间的Cahn-Hilliard方程模拟，具有良好的应用前景。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with a structure-preserving neural network-based framework for the Cahn-Hilliard equation in mixed form. We employ generalized transferable neural networks (GTransNet) for spatial approximation and stabilized backward differentiation formulas (BDF) for temporal discretization. The resulting first- and second-order in time GTransNet-BDF schemes are shown to conserve mass and satisfy energy stability at the time-discrete level. The schemes are implemented by a collocation-based method, in which a least-squares system with constant coefficient matrix needs to be solved at each time step. The solution of this system, which determines the output-layer weights of the network, violates mass conservation due to the expected nonzero least-squares residual. To overcome this issue, we introduce a novel post-processing mass-conserving projection that enforces the mass constraint through a minimization problem, whose solution can be computed at negligible computational cost. A key advantage of the proposed method lies in its predetermined hidden layers and mesh-free nature, making the method applicable to complex domains, variable mobility, and long-time simulations. Extensive numerical experiments in two and three dimensions verify convergence, mass conservation, and energy dissipation as well as demonstrate the accuracy and robustness of the proposed GTransNet-BDF schemes.

</details>


### [99] [Quadrilateral and Hexahedral Immersed Finite Element Methods for Elliptic Interface Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24055)
*Fangfang Qin,Haifeng Ji*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于四边形和六面体网格的节点等参浸入有限元（IFE）方法，通过合理选择离散通量条件的施加点，解决了张量扩散系数下的基函数唯一可解性问题，并保持了最优逼近性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于三角形或矩形网格的节点IFE方法在处理张量扩散系数时存在基函数不唯一可解的问题，且受角度条件限制，难以适用于复杂几何和各向异性材料。本文旨在克服这些局限性。

Method: 提出一种节点等参IFE方法，在四边形和六面体单元上构造IFE空间，利用非恒定离散通量的特性，系统地选择通量连续性条件的施加点以保证基函数的唯一可解性，并证明其最优逼近性能。

Result: 理论分析和数值实验均表明，该方法对任意形状的四边形和六面体网格均能保证IFE基函数的唯一可解性，适用于标量和张量扩散系数，并实现了最优收敛阶。

Conclusion: 所提方法突破了传统IFE方法的角度限制和仅适用于标量系数的局限，为界面问题在非匹配网格上的高效求解提供了更灵活、稳健的工具。

Abstract: Immersed finite element (IFE) methods provide an effective framework for solving interface problems on unfitted meshes. The basic idea underlying IFE methods is to modify standard finite element spaces by enforcing interface conditions at certain points. It is known that, for nodal degrees of freedom, unisolvence of linear IFE basis functions on triangular elements is subject to a non-obtuse-angle condition for scalar diffusion coefficients. This limitation is more severe for tensor diffusion coefficients, for which the non-obtuse-angle condition is no longer sufficient for unisolvence. Even on rectangular meshes, conventional bilinear IFE basis functions may not be unisolvent for tensor diffusion coefficients. In this paper, we develop and analyze a nodal isoparametric IFE method on quadrilateral and hexahedral meshes and show that the unisolvence issue can be overcome by appropriately selecting the enforcement point of the discrete flux condition. The key observation is that, unlike triangular elements, the discrete flux in quadrilateral and hexahedral elements is not constant, which provides the flexibility to select such an enforcement point to ensure unisolvence. We provide a systematic procedure for this selection that not only ensures unisolvence of the IFE basis functions on general quadrilateral and hexahedral elements with either scalar or tensor-valued diffusion coefficients but also preserves the optimal approximation properties of the resulting IFE space. The proposed IFE method offers several advantages: flexibility for complex geometries, the absence of angle restrictions, and applicability to tensor diffusion coefficients, thereby overcoming the limitations of existing nodal IFE methods on rectangular and triangular meshes. Optimal error estimates are established and confirmed by numerical experiments.

</details>


### [100] [Derivative-Free Recovery of a Nonlinearity in a Free-Boundary DCIS Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24090)
*De-Han Chen,Hongyu Liu,Keji Liu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 研究了一种多维自由边界DCIS模型中的逆系数问题，通过针吸活检获得的营养浓度时间序列恢复未知的营养消耗函数，并提出了一种结合线性收敛Picard迭代与立方Sperner搜索的同伦延拓方法进行求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在从临床可获取的数据（如针吸活检的营养浓度轨迹）中反演肿瘤生长模型中的关键参数（营养消耗函数），以提高对导管原位癌（DCIS）发展过程的定量理解与个性化建模能力。

Method: 将正问题建立在适定的消耗函数类上，证明局部适定性；将逆问题转化为不动点问题，采用有限维逼近构造离散迭代算子，并设计结合Picard迭代与立方Sperner搜索的同伦延拓法求解，避免了目标泛函微分和伴随状态计算。

Result: 证明了离散不动点的存在性及其子列强收敛至连续算子不动点，该不动点在一致性条件下解决逆问题；数值实验验证了理论结果，在径向对称与非对称DCIS模型中均取得良好效果。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效求解基于自由边界模型的DCIS营养消耗函数逆问题，为无需梯度信息和伴随计算的生物医学参数反演提供了新途径。

Abstract: We investigate an inverse coefficient problem for a multidimensional free-boundary model of ductal carcinoma in situ (DCIS), in which the tumor interface is governed by the nonlinear coupling of nutrient concentration, tissue pressure and curvature, and the unknown nutrient consumption function is recovered from a temporal trace of the nutrient concentration obtained by needle aspiration biopsy. For the forward problem, we establish uniform local well-posedness over an admissible class of consumption functions. The inverse problem is recast as a fixed-point problem: approximating the admissible set by finite-dimensional spaces yields discrete iteration operators, for which we prove the existence of fixed points, and the strong convergence of a subsequence of discrete fixed points to a fixed point of the continuous operator, which solves the inverse problem under a consistency condition. To approximate these fixed points, we develop a homotopy-continuation method combining a linearly convergent Picard iteration with a cubical Sperner search, without differentiating an objective functional or computing an adjoint state. Several numerical experiments on radially symmetric and non-symmetric DCIS models corroborate the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [101] [Strang's Conjecture: Positive Result on Strong Collapsible Complexes and a Code to Check the Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24120)
*Aranzazu Romero,Renaldi-Bradley Bodombo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了双变量C^1样条函数空间的维数问题，通过引入相关函数空间和内蕴算子，给出了Strang猜想的一个等价表述，并提供了用于验证该猜想的NGSolve代码；此外，证明了在三角剖分对应的单纯复形强可缩（且满足非共线条件）时，Strang猜想在k=2情况下成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Strang猜想关于双变量C^1样条空间维数的预测在k=2时存在反例，因此需要更深入理解其成立条件，尤其是低次情况下的结构特性。

Method: 将C^1样条空间与其它函数空间建立联系，利用内蕴算子构造一个复形，从而得到Strang猜想的等价形式，并结合计算工具（NGSolve）进行验证；同时使用拓扑方法分析单纯复形的可缩性。

Result: 得到了Strang猜想的一个等价表述，提出了可用于检验猜想的计算方法，并证明当三角剖分对应的单纯复形强可缩且满足非共线条件时，Strang猜想在k=2时成立。

Conclusion: 通过代数与拓扑方法的结合，为Strang猜想在k=2情形提供了新的充分条件，并推动了对样条空间维数结构的理解。

Abstract: Splines, which are piecewise polynomial functions with given smoothness, are used in numerous applications such as computer aided geometric design, algebraic geometry and for implementation of the finite element method. In many applications, it is important to know the dimension of the space of splines over a domain, but this continues to be an open problem. Strang's conjecture predicts the dimension of the space of bivariate $C^1$ splines of degree at most $k$ over a polygonal domain with no holes. For the cases, $k=4$ and $k \geq 5$ the conjecture has been proven true. For the case $k=2$, John Morgan and L. Ridgway Scott provided a counterexample. In this project, we begin by relating the space of bivariate $C^1$ splines of degree at most $k$ to various other function spaces. With the aid of some intrinsic operators on these spaces, we obtain a complex that yields an equivalence statement to Strang's conjecture. The equivalent statement admits a computational approach to the conjecture and thus we present an NGSolve code to check Strang's conjecture for a given mesh. In addition, we prove that if the simplicial complex associated to a triangulation is strongly collapsible (with a non-collinear condition), then Strang's conjecture holds for $k=2$.

</details>


### [102] [Condition numbers of block Toeplitz matrices and stability of space-time IgA approximations for the wave and Schrödinger equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24151)
*Manuel Bogoya,Albrecht Böttcher,Matteo Ferrari,Sergei M Grudsky,Stefano Serra-Capizzano*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了块Toeplitz矩阵的条件数行为，并将其应用于时空Galerkin方法的稳定性分析，揭示了条件数可能指数增长的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展对带状Toeplitz矩阵条件数的研究至块Toeplitz矩阵，并分析其在高正则性等几何时间步进方法中的稳定性影响。

Method: 通过理论推导获得条件数的上下界，并结合具体数值实验验证结果。

Result: 证明了即使符号生成Fredholm算子，有限矩阵的条件数仍可能至少指数增长；给出了快速增长的例子并应用于空间-时间Galerkin方法的稳定性分析。

Conclusion: 块Toeplitz矩阵的条件数可能严重恶化，影响数值方法的稳定性，需进一步研究以解决相关开放问题。

Abstract: In previous work by several authors, the behavior of the condition numbers of banded Toeplitz matrices was studied as the matrix size tends to infinity. In the present contribution, two main directions are pursued. As a first step, we extend this study to block Toeplitz matrices with blocks of fixed size $N$. As in the scalar case, we show that even when the symbol generates a Fredholm infinite Toeplitz operator, the condition numbers of the finite matrices may grow at least exponentially. Upper and lower bounds for the condition numbers are obtained, and examples showing that they may grow arbitrarily fast are presented. Then, as a second step, we apply the developed theory to the stability analysis of space-time Galerkin methods, where in time an Isogeometric approach is used with regularity $r$, $1\le r\le p-1$, $p$ being the employed polynomial degree. These stability issues are related exactly to the conditioning of block Toeplitz-like matrices with blocks of size $N=p-r$. Specific examples are treated in detail and related numerical experiments are presented and critically discussed. We finally present a short list of relevant open problems.

</details>


### [103] [Stability, Convergence, and Error Analysis of Finite Element Methods for 3D Magnetohydrodynamics with $p$-Laplacian Viscosity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24197)
*Kim Ngan Le,Akash Ashirbad Panda,Evana Islam Sarkar*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于三维不可压缩磁流体动力学（MHD）流动的全离散有限元方法，适用于具有非线性p-拉普拉斯粘性的牛顿和非牛顿剪切增稠流体，证明了收敛性并给出了无条件误差估计，数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展有限元方法以处理具有非线性p-拉普拉斯粘性的三维不可压缩MHD流动，涵盖非牛顿剪切增稠流体，并克服时间步长与网格尺寸之间的耦合限制。

Method: 结合空间有限元与半隐式欧拉时间离散化，采用时间平移紧致性论证和Minty单调性方法证明收敛性，并在额外正则性假设下推导无条件误差估计。

Result: 证明了格式收敛到弱解，获得了速度场和磁场的无条件误差估计，数值模拟验证了理论收敛速率并展示了方法的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方法成功扩展了不可压缩MHD系统的有限元分析至非牛顿流体领域，同时兼容经典牛顿流体情形，具备良好的理论保证和数值表现。

Abstract: This paper develops a fully discrete finite element method for three-dimensional incompressible magnetohydrodynamic (MHD) flows with nonlinear $p$-Laplace viscosity. The scheme combines spatial finite elements with a semi-implicit Euler time discretisation. Convergence to a weak solution is proved using a time-translation compactness argument together with Minty's monotonicity method. Under additional regularity assumptions, we derive unconditional error estimates for both the velocity and magnetic field, with no coupling restriction between the time step and mesh size. The framework extends finite element analysis of incompressible MHD systems to non-Newtonian shear-thickening fluids ($p>2$), while recovering the classical Newtonian case ($p=2$). Finally, numerical simulations are provided to validate the theoretical convergence rates and demonstrate the robustness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [104] [Eigenvalue bounds for preconditioned symmetric multiple saddle-point matrices with block-triangular preconditioners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24203)
*Luca Bergamaschi,Michele Bergamaschi,John W. Pearson*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了基于近似Schur补的块三角预处理矩阵对称块三对角多重鞍点线性系统的特征值界，证明了复特征值严格包含在复平面上以1为中心的圆内，实正特征值由一系列参数多项式的极值根界定，数值结果表明该界能很好描述预处理矩阵的特征值分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更好地理解和控制多重鞍点问题中预处理后矩阵的特征值分布，提升迭代求解效率。

Method: 利用基于近似Schur补的块三角预处理技术，结合参数多项式分析实特征值，并通过理论推导确定复特征值的有界区域。

Result: 证明了所有具有非零虚部的复特征值严格位于复平面以1为中心的圆内，实正特征值被参数多项式的极值根所界定，且数值实验验证了理论界的准确性。

Conclusion: 所提出的特征值界有效刻画了预处理后矩阵的谱分布，为多重鞍点系统的高效求解提供了理论支持。

Abstract: We develop eigenvalue bounds for symmetric, block-tridiagonal multiple saddle-point linear systems, preconditioned with block-triangular matrices, based on approximate Schur complements. Irrespective on the number of blocks, we prove that all complex eigenvalues, with nontrivial imaginary part, are strictly contained in a circle within the complex plane, with center 1. The real and positive eigenvalues are bounded in terms of the extremal roots of a sequences of parametric polynomials. Numerical results reveal that the bounds describe very well the eigenvalue distribution of the preconditioned matrix.

</details>


### [105] [Robust training and rigorous error analysis of physics-informed neural networks for the $p$-Laplace equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24205)
*Kyueon Choi,Seungchan Ko,Dohyun Kwon*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于求解非线性偏微分方程的物理信息神经网络（PINNs）的鲁棒训练框架，并针对p-Laplace方程设计了新的损失函数，结合对偶残差损失和边界损失，实现了先验和后验误差估计，且扩展至参数化情形，数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数PINN的理论分析局限于线性方程，非线性问题上与经典数值分析之间存在较大差距，本文旨在填补这一空白，提供针对非线性PDE的严格误差分析和鲁棒训练方法。

Method: 针对p-Laplace方程，提出一种结合W^{-1,p'}空间中的对偶残差损失和W^{1-1/p,p}范数下的边界损失的新损失形式，以适应弱解的正则性限制，并在此基础上建立a priori和a posteriori误差估计；进一步将框架推广到参数化设置。

Result: 建立了PINNs求解非线性PDE的严格误差估计理论，包括先验和后验误差界；在参数变化情况下仍保持有效性；数值实验结果支持所提方法的理论分析。

Conclusion: 该研究为PINNs在非线性偏微分方程上的应用提供了坚实的理论基础，缩小了其与传统数值方法之间的理论差距，展示了其在复杂、参数化问题中的潜力。

Abstract: With the rise of scientific machine learning, physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have been extensively applied to a wide range of problems. Nevertheless, most theoretical analyses of PINNs remain confined to linear equations, and a substantial gap persists between PINNs and classical numerical analysis for nonlinear problems. To address this issue, we propose a robust training framework for PINNs solving nonlinear partial differential equations, together with a rigorous error analysis. Specifically, for the $p$-Laplace equation, we introduce a novel loss formulation that combines a dual residual loss measured in $W^{-1,p'}$ with a boundary loss measured in a fractional Sobolev norm $W^{1-\frac{1}{p},p}$. This formulation is designed to accommodate the limited regularity of weak solutions and enables us to establish rigorous \textit{a priori} and \textit{a posteriori} error estimates. Moreover, the proposed framework and its analysis are extended to a parametric setting in which the exponent, the source term, and the boundary condition may all vary with the parameters. Finally, we present numerical experiments that substantiate our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [106] [Samplet compression for conditionally positive definite kernels and universal Kriging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24283)
*Sara Avesani,Rüdiger Kempf,Michael Multerer,Holger Wendland*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于samplet的框架，用于高效求解条件正定核逼近中的鞍点系统，具有O(N log N)计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决通用和普遍Kriging中由条件正定核逼近产生的鞍点系统的数值求解效率问题。

Method: 利用samplet的消失矩性质及其与离散正交多项式相关的缩放分布结构，对鞍点系统进行自然的零空间约简，转化为一个大型线性系统和一个小的三角系统，并采用稀疏Cholesky分解求解。

Result: 实现了鞍点系统的O(N log N)组装和存储成本，约简后的系统对称正定，可高效求解；给出了Beppo-Levi空间中多调和样条逼近的误差界。

Conclusion: 该框架在保持高精度的同时显著提升了计算效率，适用于高维数据建模与大规模问题，如高斯过程回归、图像配准和三维网格变形。

Abstract: We present a samplet-based framework for the efficient numerical solution of saddle-point systems arising from conditionally positive definite (CPD) kernel approximation in general and universal Kriging in particular. The vanishing moment property of samplets as well as the particular structure of the associated scaling distributions, which correspond to discrete orthogonal polynomials, allow for a numerically favorable representation of these saddle-point systems. Concretely, they enable a natural null-space reduction of the indefinite saddle-point system to a (large) linear system for the detail coefficients and a small triangular system for the polynomial coefficients. We derive error bounds for the approximation by polyharmonic splines in Beppo-Levi spaces and show that the detail coefficients span precisely the subspace on which the CPD kernel is positive definite, rendering the reduced block symmetric positive definite. In view of the quasi-sparsity of the samplet-transformed kernel matrix for asymptotically smooth kernels, the resulting method achieves O(N log N) cost for the assembly and the storage of the saddle-point system. The reduced system can efficiently be solved by a sparse Cholesky factorization. We illustrate the framework with three applications, namely Gaussian process regression with generalized covariances, landmark-based image registration via samplet-compressed thin plate splines, and three-dimensional mesh deformation.

</details>


### [107] [A High-Accuracy Numerical Homogenization Framework for Quasiperiodic Hamilton--Jacobi Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24313)
*Kai Jiang,Meng Li,Juan Zhang,Lei Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于计算具有凸哈密顿量的拟周期哈密顿-雅可比方程有效哈密顿量的数值均匀化框架，引入了拟周期边界条件和SL-FPR格式，并证明了其稳定性和误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拟周期哈密顿-雅可比方程的有效哈密顿量计算面临全空间求解、无平移对称性、无衰减特性及低正则性等挑战，需要发展高效准确的数值方法。

Method: 引入拟周期边界条件将全空间问题转化为有界区域上的问题，结合半拉格朗日近似与有限点恢复方法（SL-FPR），建立稳定性与误差估计，并将均匀化结果从二次情形推广到一般的k>1情况。

Result: 所提SL-FPR方法在数值实验中展现出良好的收敛性和适用性，能够精确逼近对应的有效哈密顿量，并验证了推广后的均匀化理论结果。

Conclusion: 该研究为拟周期哈密顿-雅可比方程的有效哈密顿量计算提供了一个稳定、准确且可推广的数值框架。

Abstract: In this work, we develop an accurate numerical homogenization framework for computing effective Hamiltonians of quasiperiodic Hamilton--Jacobi equations (QHJEs) with convex Hamiltonians of the form $H(x,p) = |p|^k/{k}-f(x), ~k>1$, where $f$ is quasiperiodic. Computing effective Hamiltonians in the quasiperiodic setting requires solving QHJEs posed on the whole space. Their solutions generally possess neither translational symmetry nor decay and may exhibit low regularity. These features pose substantial challenges for numerical computation. To address these difficulties, we introduce a quasiperiodic boundary condition, which allows the original whole-space problem to be treated on a bounded domain while preserving quasiperiodicity at the boundary. We then propose an SL--FPR scheme that combines a semi-Lagrangian approximation with the finite points recovery method and establish stability and error estimates for the resulting scheme. We also extend the quasiperiodic homogenization result from the quadratic case to general $k>1$ and apply the proposed method to accurately approximate the corresponding effective Hamiltonians. Numerical experiments illustrate the convergence and applicability of the method and validate the extended homogenization results.

</details>


### [108] [Efficient Hermitian and skew-Hermitian splitting methods for linear systems in micromagnetic simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24576)
*Yingxi Miao,Changjian Xie*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了Landau-Lifshitz方程的半隐式方法所导致的大型稀疏非厄米但正定线性系统，采用HSS及其非精确变体IHSS方法求解，并通过数值实验验证了方法的有效性和收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了高效求解Landau-Lifshitz方程半隐式离散后产生的大型稀疏非厄米正定线性系统。

Method: 应用Hermitian/斜Hermitian分裂（HSS）方法及其非精确形式（IHSS）来求解离散后的线性系统，并分析其收敛行为。

Result: 数值实验表明，HSS迭代的谱半径始终低于理论上界且小于1，理论估计能准确反映实际收敛行为；IHSS在测试案例中表现出有效收敛，但其效率对分裂参数敏感。两种半隐式格式具有相似的收敛表现。

Conclusion: HSS和IHSS方法适用于求解该类离散系统，具有良好的收敛性能，其中理论边界能准确预测实际收敛速度，且方法对参数选择有一定依赖性。

Abstract: For the Landau-Lifshitz equation, the discrete linear systems obtained by our semi-implicit method possess the following properties: they are large-sparse systems with non-Hermitian yet positive-definite coefficient matrices. To solve these systems efficiently, we apply the Hermitian/skew-Hermitian splitting (HSS) method and its inexact variant (IHSS). Numerical experiments in one and three dimensions show that the spectral radius of the HSS iteration remains below its theoretical upper bound and strictly below one for the tested grid resolutions and damping parameters. Moreover, the theoretical bound closely follows the actual spectral radius, providing an accurate estimate of the convergence behavior. The IHSS results demonstrate effective convergence for the tested cases and show that its efficiency is sensitive to the splitting parameter. Overall, the two semi-implicit schemes exhibit comparable convergence behavior.

</details>


### [109] [Real-time inverse solutions via neural matrix operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24833)
*Julie Pham,Thomas O'Leary-Roseberry,Omar Ghattas,Karen Willcox*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为NEural Matrix Operator (NEMO)的降维神经算子方法，用于在数字孪生中实现实时逆问题求解，特别适用于由非线性参数化PDE控制的时空动力系统。NEMO通过学习从模型参数到观测算子的非线性映射，在低维空间中实现快速闭式逆求解，显著加速了数据同化过程，并具备实时不确定性量化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为满足数字孪生中对实时预测与控制的需求，亟需快速的数据同化方法。传统基于物理求解器的逆问题求解方法计算成本高，难以满足实时性要求。

Method: 提出NEMO方法，利用神经网络学习从非线性模型参数m到参数-观测量算子F(m)的映射，输出为低维空间中的矩阵形式算子；利用该算子，对新的m可实现关于线性反演参数q的闭式逆问题求解。

Result: 在污染物输运初值识别和高超声速飞行器载荷识别两个实际应用中，NEMO相比传统PDE求解器实现了三个数量级以上的加速，且逆问题求解质量高；与先进的多输入神经算子相比，性能相当但在线计算复杂度降低一个数量级以上，并支持实时不确定性量化。

Conclusion: NEMO为物理约束下的实时逆问题求解提供了一种高效、可扩展的新范式，显著提升了数字孪生系统中的数据同化速度与实用性。

Abstract: Rapid data assimilation is required for real-time prediction and control in digital twins. For many physical systems, the data assimilation task requires the solution of a physics-constrained inverse problem, which is often computationally intractable in real time using traditional physics solvers. This work presents a reduced-basis neural operator approach to enable real-time inverse problem solutions in the digital twin setting. Our approach specifically targets the large class of problems with spatiotemporal dynamics governed by partial differential equations (PDEs) that are parameterized nonlinearly with respect to model parameters $m$, and linearly with respect to inversion parameters $q$. Based on this physical structure, our neural operator approximates the nonlinear map from the model parameters $m$ to the parameter-to-observable operator $\mathcal{F}(m)$ in a reduced subspace. Since the output of the neural operator is the parameter-to-observable operator itself (manifested as a matrix), we refer to this approach as NEural Matrix Operator (NEMO). With NEMO, for new given $m$, we enable a closed-form inverse problem solution for $q$ in a reduced subspace. We apply NEMO in two real-world applications: contaminant transport initial condition identification, and hypersonic vehicle load identification. We show that NEMO delivers high quality inverse problem solutions for data assimilation in real time, with over three orders of magnitude speedup compared to constructing the reduced operator with the PDE solver. Further, NEMO demonstrates comparable inverse performance to a state-of-the-art multiple-input neural operator, while reducing online computational complexity by over an order of magnitude and providing real-time uncertainty quantification.

</details>

### [110] [NatPar: Natural Parametric Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24871)
*Hirbod Assa*

Main category: q-fin.RM

TL;DR: 本文提出了自然参数保险（NatPar），借鉴巨灾保险行业的现有操作方式，通过参数化指数合同替代传统的损失调整，标准化报告格式并重新分配尾部风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在提出一种实用的、标准化的参数保险方法，解决传统保险中损失调整的复杂性，并借鉴巨灾保险模型的成熟框架。

Method: 采用灾害-暴露-脆弱性-金融模型框架，引入参数化指数合同，使用年平均损失（AAL）、EP曲线和回报期水平等标准指标进行报告，并提出双侧基础偏离诊断（BEP+/-）作为核心工具评估基差风险。

Result: 研究表明，在保持合同AAL中性的情况下，有界赔付无法跟随无界暴露尾部，导致短周期内投保人获益而保险人规避深尾风险；但在尾部依赖情况下，多个区域极端事件同时发生时，赔付叠加使保险人重新承担深尾风险。

Conclusion: NatPar提供了一种可标准化的参数保险设计路径，明确揭示了基差风险的分布特征及尾部风险在不同情境下的再分配机制，有助于提升参数保险的透明度与实用性。

Abstract: We develop natural parametric (NatPar) insurance as the natural next step from natural-catastrophe (NatCat) modelling: the same hazard-exposure-vulnerability-finance machinery, with a parametric index made contractual in place of indemnity loss adjustment. Our aim is practical - a standard approach inspired by how the catastrophe-insurance industry already operates, not another optimal-contract criterion. This delivers two payoffs. First, it fixes how reporting is formulated: NatPar contracts are reported in the native NatCat language (annual average loss, EP/AEP/OEP curves, return-period levels), complemented with two-sided basis-exceedance diagnostics (BEP+/-) elevated to the central status the EP curve holds for losses - the canonical distributional view of basis risk, not a supplementary number. Second, the same standard shows how the tail is reallocated between insuree and insurer.
  A frost case study yields the central result: it is about time, not average. Holding a contract AAL-neutral, the bounded payout cannot follow the unbounded exposure tail, so equalising the mean separates over- and under-payment across return periods: the insuree gains at short horizons while the insurer sheds the deep tail past a crossover of several decades. This reverses under tail dependence - when regions reach extremes jointly, bounded payouts stack and the insurer reabsorbs the deep tail.

</details>

### [111] [Single-shot online sequence classification with unbounded quantum memory advantage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23669)
*Keith K. Ng,Haochen Jay Li,Mile Gu,Jayne Thompson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类在线多分类任务，证明了在这些任务中，任何精确的经典智能体需要无限增长的内存，而量子智能体可以用有限内存完成所有任务，实现了经典与量子内存开销之间的无界分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究复杂环境中序列决策问题的经典与量子智能体在内存需求上的根本差异，探索量子计算在在线学习任务中的潜在优势。

Method: 构建一类在线多分类游戏，分析经典和量子智能体在执行这些任务时所需的最小内存，并通过理论证明比较其内存复杂度。

Result: 证明了在特定输入分布下，任何内存不足的经典智能体会表现接近随机猜测，而存在使用有界内存的量子智能体可以精确完成所有任务；并得出该类任务的精确经典与量子内存复杂度。

Conclusion: 在在线多分类任务中存在无界的经典与量子内存开销分离，表明量子智能体在处理某些持续学习任务时具有根本性的内存效率优势。

Abstract: An agent monitors a complex environment, receiving one observation at each time step and eventually deciding how to label the resulting sequence. Does the sequence indicate an anomaly, and if so, of what type? Does it signal market instability, and to what degree? This is the setting of online multi-class classification: the input arrives sequentially, the full history is never available at once, and the agent must retain any past information relevant to the eventual decision. As the environment becomes more complex, the memory needed to track this information can grow rapidly without bound. Here, we introduce families of such multi-class classification games and show that any exact classical agent requires memory that grows without bound, whereas exact quantum agents can solve all tasks in the family with bounded memory. This separation is sharp: any classical agent using less than the required memory, under suitable input distributions, performs arbitrarily close to random guessing. Moreover, our quantum constructions are provably memory minimal, allowing us to derive the exact classical and quantum memory complexities to perform such tasks. In doing so, we establish an unbounded separation between classical and quantum memory cost for online multi-class classification.

</details>


### [112] [Bang-bang protocol for nondispersive qubit readout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23681)
*Nina del Ser,Yinan Chen,Jacob Steiner,Gil Refael*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种称为“bang-bang读出”的新型超导量子比特非破坏性读出协议，通过突然淬灭耦合常数并利用预加载光子的谐振腔，在非色散 regime 实现高速、高保真度的单次投影测量，读出误差随光子数N增加而降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统色散 regime 读出速度受限于弱耦合，难以兼顾高速与高QND性；非色散 regime 虽可提升速率但易因参数扫描引发测量诱导跃迁（MIST），需寻找更优控制方案。

Method: 提出bang-bang读出协议：通过突然改变耦合强度而非连续扫参，结合预加载光子或强驱动（如通量驱动）实现快速投影测量；发展了Jaynes-Cummings模型的动力学解析理论，系统展开1/√N修正项以分析性能。

Result: 实现了接近理想单次投影测量，读出保真度和QND性极高，误差随1/N下降；理论表明读出速率可达~g，显著优于传统色散方法的~g²√N/Δ；并验证了无需预加载光子、改用强驱动亦可实现该协议。

Conclusion: bang-bang读出协议突破了传统读出在速度与QND性之间的权衡，为高保真量子传感与计算提供了高效、鲁棒的测量新范式。

Abstract: Fast, precise, and quantum-non-demolition (QND) readout of superconducting qubits is a fundamental component of high-fidelity quantum sensing and computation. Conventional approaches typically operate in the dispersive regime, where the qubit-resonator coupling $g$ is weak compared to the detuning $Δ$. While exhibiting good QND properties, the readout rate is limited to $\sim g^2\sqrt{N}/Δ\ll g$, where $N$ is the number of photons in the resonator. QND readout in the nondispersive regime, where the readout rate reaches its full potential $\sim g$, relies on parameter sweeps that may encounter resonances, leading to measurement-induced state transitions (MIST). In this work, we study a nondispersive readout protocol that replaces these sweeps by sudden quenches of the coupling constant, using a resonator that is preloaded with photons. We call this protocol bang-bang readout, and show that it realizes single-shot projective measurements. The fidelity and QNDness of the qubit post-measurement are remarkably high, with an error that decreases like $1/N$. To arrive at these findings, we develop an analytical theory for the dynamics and measurements of the Jaynes-Cummings (JC) model, including a systematic expansion of correction terms in powers of $1/\sqrt{N}$. We show that the protocol can also be implemented without preloading the resonator by instead strongly driving the qubit, e.g., with a classical flux drive.

</details>


### [113] [Compositionality in quantum reference frame perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23698)
*Bruna Sahdo,Esteban Castro-Ruiz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子参考系（QRF）视角下子系统的组合问题，利用一种新的形式框架解决了‘第三粒子悖论’等难题，提出了子系统添加与移除的一致性方法，并引入‘经典化’过程以从QRF中恢复类经典视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在将标准量子理论中的组合概念应用于量子参考系时出现的悖论和概念性问题，例如‘第三粒子悖论’，并建立一个一致的子系统组合框架。

Method: 基于Castro-Ruiz和Oreshkov提出的QRF形式框架，通过内化外部观测者的参考系，构建QRF视角的层次结构，并定义子系统的增减操作；同时分析‘额外粒子’自由度的作用，提出‘经典化’程序。

Result: 发现由于‘额外粒子’自由度的存在，QRF视角下的子系统组合不同于标准量子情况，不能任意以张量积形式添加状态，且需满足特定相容性条件；‘经典化’过程可绕过这些限制但具有不同的操作意义。

Conclusion: QRF视角下的子系统组合需要推广标准量子理论中的组合方式，该框架自然避免了悖论，并为理解复合量子系统提供了更一致的理论基础。

Abstract: Understanding a composite system through its constituents is a fundamental practice in physics. In the context of quantum reference frames (QRFs), however, combining the usual quantum-theoretic notion of composition with QRF perspectives gives rise to subtle issues, such as the 'paradox of the third particle'. Here we study in depth how to compose subsystems in QRF perspectives, building on a recent formalism for QRFs [E.Castro-Ruiz and O.Oreshkov, 2025]. We first show how the frames of external observers can be internalised and treated within the framework. This establishes a consistent hierarchy of QRF perspectives, which we use to define the adding and removing of subsystems. We then explain how, owing to the so-called 'extra-particle' degrees of freedom, the formalism gives a consistent treatment of subsystems, avoiding any paradoxes by construction. Consistency then implies that composing subsystems in a QRF perspective differs from, and generalises, the standard quantum-theoretic case. In particular, not every state can be appended in tensor-product form relative to a QRF, and we characterise the set of compatible states. Finally, we introduce 'classicalisation', a procedure that recovers a classical-like perspective from a general QRF. This procedure circumvents the aforementioned state restrictions but carries a different operational meaning, which we study through a concrete example.

</details>


### [114] [Hamiltonian Learning at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23707)
*Nathan Johnson,Eugene Dumitrescu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究利用张量网络技术模拟含噪声的大规模哈密顿学习流程，提出基于近似稳态的哈密顿学习方法，并分析其在误差下的稳定性，成功应用于300个自旋的混合场伊辛模型，为未来实验提供了重要指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解哈密顿学习协议在不可避免误差下的容错能力（即稳定性）对于实际量子系统的控制和应用至关重要。

Method: 采用基于矩阵乘积态工具构建的近似稳态进行哈密顿学习，并结合张量网络技术模拟含噪声的学习流程，提供解析误差界并开展数值模拟。

Result: 给出了哈密顿估计误差的解析界限；数值模拟显示学习误差在两类误差下具有稳定性；成功将协议扩展到300个格点的混合场伊辛模型。

Conclusion: 大规模模拟揭示了哈密顿学习的实际局限性和误差影响，为未来实验设计提供了有价值的参考，并指出了进一步支持哈密顿学习实验的研究方向。

Abstract: Learning a quantum system's Hamiltonian is crucial for understanding and controlling its dynamics and has recently become a topic of widespread interest. To understand the learning protocol's error tolerances, i.e. its stability in the presence of inevitable errors, this work utilizes tensor network techniques to emulate Hamiltonian learning workflows at scale and with noise. Specifically, we employed a Hamiltonian learning technique based on approximate stationary states which are constructed using matrix product tools. We provide analytic bounds on the Hamiltonian learning estimation errors and perform numerical simulations that highlight learning error's stability under two families of errors. Using our workflow, we are able to scale up the protocol and learn mixed-field Ising model Hamiltonians of an $N=300$ site spin chain. By simulating the protocol at large scale, and empirically studying its practical limitations, our analysis of how errors affect the Hamiltonian learning process provides valuable lessons for future experiments. We conclude by discussing the avenues our work opens as well as future work that can support Hamiltonian learning experiments.

</details>


### [115] [Provable Quantum--Classical Separation for Continuous Gibbs Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24527)
*Enrico Olivucci,Mariia Sobchuk,Sehmimul Hoque,Jeffrey Hnybida,Kyungho W. Kim,Ala Shayeghi,Pooya Ronagh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了在连续域上采样问题的首个量子-经典分离。对于一类定义在环面T^d上的吉布斯态，任何经典算法需要Ω(α)次查询才能以恒定精度采样，而量子算法仅需Õ(√α)次查询，实现了在势垒幅度上的二次加速，在低温下维度增加时可转化为指数级优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子算法相对于经典算法在连续域采样问题上的潜在优势，特别是在处理具有高势垒的多峰分布时经典方法效率低下的问题。

Method: 利用量子奇异值阈值和温度退火技术设计量子算法；通过信息论下界分析证明经典算法所需的最小查询次数。

Result: 建立了首个连续域采样问题的量子-经典复杂性分离，量子算法查询复杂度为Õ(√α)，优于经典的Ω(α)，并在低温度下随维度呈指数级优势。

Conclusion: 在光滑势能的吉布斯采样问题中，量子算法相比所有基于查询的经典算法具有显著且可量化的优越性，展示了量子计算在连续问题中的潜力。

Abstract: We prove the first quantum--classical separation for a sampling problem over a continuous domain. For a class of Gibbs states $p\propto e^{-βE}$ on the torus $\mathbb{T}^d$ with smooth ($s$-Gevrey) potential and barrier amplitude $α=e^{βΔ}$, where $Δ= \max E-\min E$, every classical algorithm---querying the value, gradient, or any higher-order derivatives of the log-density---requires $Ω(α)$ queries to sample at constant accuracy in total variation distance, while a quantum algorithm based on quantum singular value thresholding and temperature annealing samples with $\tilde{O}\left(\sqrtα\right)$ queries to an oracle for the gradient. The advantage is quadratic in the barrier amplitude, which becomes exponential in the dimension, $e^{Ω(d)}$, at low temperature. The classical bound is information-theoretic, holding for every classical algorithm with query access to the Gibbs potential and its derivatives at any order.

</details>


### [116] [Sustained macroscopic quantum coherence in a superradiant solid under ambient conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23748)
*Wei-Jiang Wu,Da-Wu Xiao,Wen-Tao Wang,Xin-Yu Chen,Xian-Feng Wang,Ming-Zhong Ai,Quan Li,Ren-Bao Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究在常温下实现了固态超辐射微波激射器，展示了大量氮空位中心自旋与微波光子之间的长寿命宏观量子相干，为探索具有多体关联的亮量子光源提供了新平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现宏观量子相干在基础物理和量子技术中具有重要意义，但此前超辐射的实现受限于气体系统、极低温固体或短脉冲条件，亟需在更实用条件下实现固态系统中的持续相干。

Method: 利用钻石中的氮空位中心自旋体系与微波腔耦合，构建固态超辐射微波激射器，并通过调节系统参数进入不同超阈值 regime，观测相应的相干现象。

Result: 在常温下实现了包含约10^14个自旋和10^9个光子的长寿命宏观量子相干，观察到连续波激射、周期性振幅调制和超辐射爆发序列，分别对应固定频率同步、大自旋时间晶体和非同步超辐射瞬态。

Conclusion: 宏观量子相干可在常温固态系统中自发产生并维持，为基于固态平台的量子科技和多体量子现象研究开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Macroscopic quantum coherence, such as in laser, Bose-Einstein condensates, superfluids, and superconductors, is important to fundamental physics and useful for quantum technologies. Superradiance provides a mechanism to produce coherence among a large number of particles and photons. Its implementation, however, has been limited to gaseous systems, solids at very low temperature, or short pulses. Here we demonstrate a solid-state superradiant maser under ambient conditions, which establishes long-lived coherence among about $10^{14}$ nitrogen-vacancy center spins in diamond and about $10^9$ photons in a microwave cavity. By varying the system parameters to access the above-threshold, well-above-threshold, and deep-above-threshold regimes, we observed continuous-wave masing, periodic amplitude modulation, and sequences of superradiant bursts, which are attributed, correspondingly, to macroscopic spin coherence synchronized at a fixed frequency, a coherent time crystal of large spins, and unsynchronized superradiant transients. This work demonstrates that macroscopic quantum coherence can be spontaneously generated and maintained in solids under ambient conditions and provides a solid-state platform for exploring bright quantum lights with many-body correlations.

</details>


### [117] [CircLS: Compiling Lattice Surgery to Physical Circuits with Dynamic Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23819)
*John Yuehan Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: CircLS是一种将Pauli积测量序列高效降低到Stim电路的工具，实现了从量子程序到物理电路级的完整编译流程，并通过动态数据块分配显著减少了时空资源消耗和逻辑错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于格子手术的量子计算编译器停留在逻辑层面，缺乏向物理电路层级的可靠降低方法，导致无法在电路级验证程序和评估逻辑错误率。

Method: 提出线性时间的稳定子构造规则，将Pauli积测量序列降低为Stim电路，并开发支持动态数据块分配与复用的编译器。

Result: 相比先前工具链，CircLS减少了5.5倍的时空体积占用，并将逻辑错误率降低了14倍。

Conclusion: CircLS成功打通了从高层量子程序到可运行物理电路的编译流程，在资源效率和容错性能上均有显著提升，且已开源。

Abstract: In fault-tolerant quantum computing, lattice surgery (LS) is one of the leading ways to realize logical operations, and the Pauli product measurement (PPM) is the basic instruction of LS-based computing. Compilers on the PPM sequence, however, stay at the logical level rather than the physical circuit level. This is because the lowering is complicated: PPMs differ widely from each other, and each must be realized on the physical circuit without breaking fault tolerance. CircLS lowers the PPM sequence to a Stim circuit through linear-time stabilizer construction rules. This completes the pipeline from a quantum program through the PPM sequence to a Stim circuit, on which the compiled program can be verified at the circuit level and its logical error rate (LER) measured. Based on the lowering, we develop a compiler that allocates data patches dynamically: each patch is allocated at its first use and freed at its last use, and the freed tiles are reused as ancilla paths. CircLS reduces the allocated spacetime volume by $5.5\times$ and the LER by $14\times$ against the prior toolchain producing runnable circuits. CircLS is open source at https://github.com/John-YuehanZhang/CircLS.

</details>


### [118] [Quantum Algorithms and Hardness for Point-Count Approximation over Finite Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23929)
*Yota Maeda,Hiroshi Yano*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种量子算法，用于近似计算有限域上Laurent多项式的解的数量，具有多项式时间复杂度和加性误差界，并证明了该问题在一般情况下是#P-难的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决在显式有限域输入模型下，现有算法无法以对数级q的多项式时间实现加性误差近似的问题，并验证van Dam关于存在高效量子算法的猜想。

Method: 利用基于有限域上特征和的点计数公式，设计了一种无需依赖代数性质预言机的量子算法，通过量子幅值估计技术实现对解数量的高效近似。

Result: 提出了一个量子算法，能够在经典比特和量子门复杂度均为poly(n, s, log q, 1/ε, log(1/δ))的情况下，以高概率输出满足加性误差界的近似解数；同时证明当支持矩阵自由变化时，该问题在随机多项式时间图灵归约下是#P-难的。

Conclusion: 量子算法在受限支持参数下能高效近似Laurent多项式在环面群上的零点个数，但随着输入结构自由度增加，问题本质变得困难，揭示了量子方法有效性与输入结构之间的权衡关系。

Abstract: We study the approximation of the number of solutions of Laurent polynomials over finite fields. For a Laurent polynomial \[f(x)=\sum_{j=1}^{s}a_jx^{u_j}\in \mathbb{F}_q[x_1^{\pm1},\ldots,x_n^{\pm1}], \] let $U$ be its augmented support matrix whose columns are $(1,u_j)$ with rank $ρ$ and $N(f) := \# \{x\in (\mathbb{F}_q^\times)^n \mid f(x)=0\}$ be its torus point count. Our first main result is a quantum algorithm that outputs $\widehat{N}(f)$ satisfying \[ |\widehat{N}(f) - N(f)| \le \varepsilon q^{n+s/2-ρ} \] with success probability $1-δ$. Provided that $ρ$ and $\|U\|_\infty$ are bounded, the algorithm runs in both classical bit and quantum gate complexity $\mathrm{poly}(n, s, \log q, 1/\varepsilon, \log(1/δ))$. It provides finer resolution than relative-error approximations in general settings. To the best of our knowledge, in the explicit finite-field input model considered here, no previous algorithm achieves this additive accuracy with running time polynomial in $\log q$. Van Dam (arXiv:quant-ph/0405081) conjectured the existence of such an algorithm under the assumption of an oracle reflecting the algebraic properties of the polynomial. In contrast, by exploiting a point-counting formula derived from character sums over finite fields, we develop an alternative approach that efficiently approximates the number of points without assuming the existence of such an oracle. As a second main result, we prove that the same approximation problem becomes $\#$P-hard under randomized polynomial-time Turing reductions when the support matrix $U$ varies freely as part of the input. Thus, taken together, our results clarify how the effectiveness of the quantum approach depends on the tradeoff between the accuracy scale and the support parameters of the input polynomial.

</details>


### [119] [QML for Quantum Sensing under Measurement-Induced Information Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23934)
*Sounak Bhowmik,Himanshu Thapliyal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了量子机器学习（QML）在氮空位（NV）中心磁力计中的应用，比较了基于经典数据的机器学习模型与利用相干量子态的量子核模型的性能，发现保持相干量子信息对提升磁传感性能至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在NISQ时代，从噪声大、采样有限且受测量限制的量子传感数据中提取可靠信息是一个重大挑战，因此需要探索QML是否能通过学习量子数据与物理信号间的非线性关系来提升参数估计能力。

Method: 将磁场感知建模为监督回归任务，比较多种经典机器学习模型（基于测量后经典数据）与量子核模型（基于测量前相干量子态）的性能，以分离测量导致的信息损失影响。

Result: 结果显示，当学习模型可直接访问相干量子信息时，QML的传感性能显著提升；而仅增加模型复杂度或改变学习范式效果有限。

Conclusion: 保持量子相干性是提升QML增强型磁传感性能的关键，需构建紧密集成量子传感器与QML模型的学习流程以应对现实约束。

Abstract: Nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers in diamond can serve as highly sensitive solid-state quantum sensors for high-sensitivity magnetometry. However, in the noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) era, extracting reliable information from noisy, finite-shot, and measurement-limited sensing data remains a considerable challenge. Whereas, quantum machine learning (QML) offers a potential path to improve parameter estimation by learning nonlinear relationships between quantum-sensing data and the underlying physical signal. In this work, we investigate the role of QML in magnetic-field estimation within an NV center-inspired magnetometry setting. We formulated magnetic field sensing as a supervised regression task. We compared the performance of several classical machine learning models trained on measurement-based classical data with that of quantum kernel-based models trained on pre-measurement coherent quantum states. Our objective is to isolate the impact of measurement-induced information loss and therefore provide a theoretical upper bound on the sensing performance. The upper bound is achievable only when coherent quantum information is directly available to the learning model. Our results show that QML-based sensing performance improves significantly with coherent quantum-state information, and not much with changes in model complexity or learning paradigm. This observation underscores the importance of learning pipelines that tightly integrate quantum sensors and QML models to enhance magnetic field sensing under realistic constraints.

</details>


### [120] [A Categorical Framework for Wave-Particle Complementarity: Continuous Observable Structures and Fourier-Pontryagin Duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23935)
*Samuel B. Soltau*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在 rigged Hilbert 空间上的范畴量子力学框架下，通过连续 dagger-Frobenius 结构和 Pontryagin 对偶性，形式化地推导了波粒二象性和德布罗意关系 $\lambda = h/p$，指出普朗克常数的取值需由物理输入（Weyl 对易关系）确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在从范畴量子力学的基本结构出发，形式化地解释波粒二象性和德布罗意关系的起源，并澄清普朗克常数在其中的角色。

Method: 利用 locally compact abelian 群 $\mathbb{R}$ 及其 Pontryagin 对偶对应的连续 dagger-Frobenius 结构表示位置与动量，通过 Fourier 变换实现对偶性，并引入含参数 $\alpha$ 的广义特征配对分析不同傅里叶变换的等价性。

Result: 揭示了 Pontryagin 对偶决定了互补性的结构形式，但普朗克常数的具体数值需由 Weyl 对易关系这一物理输入确定；由此导出了德布罗意波长公式 $\lambda = h/p$。

Conclusion: 波粒二象性的数学结构源于群及其对偶的范畴对称性，而物理常数 $\hbar$ 的设定是连接数学结构与物理现实的关键步骤。

Abstract: We present a formulation wave-particle complementarity and the de Broglie relation $λ=h/p$ within the framework of categorical quantum mechanics on rigged Hilbert spaces. Position and momentum are represented by continuous dagger-Frobenius structures associated with the locally compact abelian group $\mathbb{R}$ and its Pontryagin dual. The Fourier transform is the unitary implementation of Pontryagin duality and relates the two observable structures. We show that a general character pairing $χ_p^{(α)}(x)=\exp(ipx/α)$ yields a one-parameter family of unitarily equivalent Fourier transforms. Pontryagin duality therefore determines the structural form of the complementarity, but not the numerical value of Planck's constant. The identification $α=\hbar$ is a physical input fixed by the Weyl commutation relations. With this input, the spatial period of the character yields $λ=h/p$.

</details>


### [121] [Improved Quantum Codes with Transversal T Gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24000)
*Adam Wills*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有横向T门的量子CSS码，提出了可实现恒定码率和增长距离的新编码方案，并首次实现了在无需Clifford校正的情况下具有横向T门的渐近编码族参数突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于T门在容错量子计算中的重要性，但具备横向T门的量子码族参数多年未取得进展，因此需要扩展可实现的参数范围。

Method: 提出了一种基于可分递减单项式码的框架，并在布尔超立方体的向下封闭集上进行打孔以构造逻辑量子比特，结合加权Reed-Muller码和随机化构造方法。

Result: 推导出此类码的距离闭式表达式，构造出具有恒定码率和增长距离的新型量子码，显著拓展了可实现的多项式码率与距离范围，并首次实现γ→0。

Conclusion: 该工作大幅拓宽了具有横向T门的量子CSS码的参数空间，为容错量子计算中的魔法态蒸馏提供了更高效的编码方案。

Abstract: In this work, we study quantum CSS codes with transversal $T$ gates. Here, $T$ gate transversality is meant in the strongest sense; the application of physical $T$ to every physical qubit yields logical $T$ on every logical qubit, without Clifford corrections. Despite the importance of the $T$ gate in fault-tolerant quantum computation, the parameters of asymptotic families of such codes have not been improved since the work of Hastings and Haah in 2017, and Haah in 2018. In this work, we significantly broaden the achievable parameters of quantum code families with transversal $T$ gates, both expanding the regime of achievable polynomial rate and distance, and constructing such codes with constant rate and growing distance; this is the first time the latter has been achieved, even when allowing Clifford corrections after the transversal $T$ gate. These are also the first codes achieving $γ\to 0$ for a code with a transversal $T$ gate, where $γ$ is the overhead exponent of magic state distillation.
  To do this, we develop a framework of divisible decreasing monomial codes, punctured at a downward-closed set on the Boolean hypercube to create logical qubits. We prove a closed-form expression for the distance of such a code punctured at such a set, which may be of independent interest. We first instantiate this with an explicit construction based on weighted Reed-Muller codes, puncturing at low Hamming-weight points, and then with a randomised construction, where a small random set of points is protected from the puncturing to save quantum code distance, achieving improved parameters.

</details>


### [122] [Threshold and Parity BosonSampling in the Linear-Mode Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24008)
*Byeongseon Go,Hyunseok Jeong,Changhun Oh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在阈值和奇偶性测量下玻色子采样（BosonSampling）的计算复杂性，证明了其输出概率估计的平均情况#P-hardness，表明即使使用二进制读出，量子优势仍可能保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管玻色子采样的难度依赖于光子数分辨探测，但许多实验使用的是阈值或奇偶性测量，其计算复杂性尚未被严格刻画。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 研究集中在线性模式 regime 下，采用由相干分束器旋转诱导的傅里叶系数提取方法，从粗粒化的输出概率中提取隐藏的难解成分。

Result: 建立了阈值和奇偶性玻色子采样中典型输出概率估计的平均情况#P-hardness，所得精度界限与标准光子数分辨玻色子采样先前结果相当。

Conclusion: 光子数分辨玻色子采样的难解概率结构在自然的二元粗粒化下依然存在，即使在碰撞主导区域也成立，支持了当前实验设置下的量子优势潜力。

Abstract: BosonSampling is among the most prominent candidates for demonstrating quantum advantage. However, while the hardness of BosonSampling relies on photon-number-resolving detection, many experimentally relevant settings and applications instead use binary readout based on threshold or parity measurements, whose computational complexity has not yet been rigorously characterized. In this work, we investigate the computational complexity of BosonSampling with threshold and parity measurements in the linear-mode regime, where the number of modes scales linearly with the number of photons and is most relevant to current experiments. In particular, we establish average-case #P-hardness of estimating typical output probabilities in threshold and parity BosonSampling, a crucial ingredient in proving the classical hardness of the corresponding sampling problems. The resulting imprecision bounds match those obtained in prior hardness results for standard photon-number-resolving BosonSampling in the linear-mode regime. The key technical ingredient is a Fourier-coefficient extraction method, induced by coherent beam-splitter rotations, that extracts hidden hard components within coarse-grained output probabilities. These results indicate that the hard output-probability structure of photon-number-resolving BosonSampling can persist under natural binary coarse-grainings, even in collision-dominant regimes.

</details>


### [123] [Time-Dependent Tunneling in the Thin-Barrier Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24032)
*Tanmay Vachaspati,Frank Wilczek,Zara Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对小作用量（如高而窄势垒）量子隧穿的微扰分析方法，以势垒下面积的倒数为控制参数，研究了共振与非共振情况下隧穿概率随时间的不同增长行为，并计算了从准束缚态到连续态的含时隧穿波函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统WKB方法适用于大隧穿作用量的情况，但对小作用量（如高而窄势垒）不适用，因此需要发展新的分析方法来研究此类隧穿过程。

Method: 采用以势垒下面积的倒数为控制参数的微扰分析方法，在1+1维情况下应用于多个例子，同时计算隧穿概率和含时隧穿波函数。

Result: 在共振情况下，隧穿概率随时间呈t²增长，非共振情况下则线性增长；成功计算了粒子从准束缚态逃逸至无穷远的含时隧穿波函数。

Conclusion: 该微扰方法有效描述了小作用量下的量子隧穿动力学，揭示了不同共振条件下隧穿概率的时间演化规律，并提供了对含时波函数的完整刻画。

Abstract: The usual WKB analysis for quantum tunneling applies when the tunneling action is large, as it is for tall, wide potential barriers. In contrast we analyze tunneling when the action is small, as it is for tunneling across a tall, thin barrier. We develop a perturbative analysis where the control parameter is the inverse of the area under the potential barrier and apply our technique to several examples in $1+1$ dimensions. In resonant situations for bound particles we find that the tunneling probability grows with time as $\propto t^2$, while in non-resonant situations it grows linearly with time. We evaluate not only the tunneling probability but also the time-dependent tunneling wavefunction for a particle that escapes to infinity, {\it i.e.} from a quasi-bound state to the continuum.

</details>


### [124] [Rank and Range Criteria for Mixed-State Determination from Local Marginals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24061)
*Xinyu Qiu,Minglong Qin,Caohan Cheng,Lin Chen,Delin Chu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了混合量子态是否由其k体边缘态唯一确定的问题，提出了一种基于范围的分析方法，并在三量子比特系统中给出了不同秩下2-UDA状态的判据，进一步推广到多部分系统，应用于真多体纠缠的认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决混合量子态是否能由其k体边缘态唯一确定这一量子系统认证中的基本问题。

Method: 通过分析全局态范围的结构，提出基于范围的方法；对三量子比特态使用SLOCC自由范围分析、二次型检验和半定规划准则，并推广至一般有限维三部分及多部分系统。

Result: 证明了具有GHZ-SLOCC自由范围的三量子比特态在秩为一、三、四时是2-UDA；给出了秩二态的充要条件并简化为有限二次型检验；建立了适用于剩余情况的半定规划判据；证明秩≥5的三量子比特态不是2-UDA，并将高秩障碍推广至多部分系统；对一类信道型多部分态精确刻画了(n-1)-UDA条件；应用于真多体纠缠认证。

Conclusion: 本文建立了判断量子态由低阶边缘态唯一确定的新方法体系，揭示了不同秩与结构下2-UDA性质的存在性边界，并为多体纠缠的可靠认证提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Determining whether a mixed quantum state is uniquely determined among all states by its k-body marginals (k-UDA) is a fundamental problem in quantum system certification. We develop a range-based approach to this problem by analyzing the structure of the range of the global state. For three-qubit states, we show that states with GHZ-SLOCC-free ranges are 2-UDA at ranks one, three, and four. We derive a necessary and sufficient range criterion for rank-two 2-UDA states and reduce it to a finite quadratic-form test. To cover the remaining range configurations, we formulate an exact range-restricted semidefinite programming criterion and extend it to arbitrary finite-dimensional tripartite states. We also show that every three-qubit state of rank at least five is not 2-UDA, and further extend high-rank obstructions to multipartite systems. For a channel-based multipartite family, we characterize exactly when a state is $(n-1)$-UDA and show that lower-order marginals never suffice. Finally, we apply these results to the certification of genuine multipartite entanglement.

</details>


### [125] [Lifting connectivity bottlenecks in superconducting quantum processors via enriched native two-qubit gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24084)
*Hanyi Wang,Jingzhe Guo,Lijun Sun,Zhaohui Yang,Weizhi Tao,Xingye Yuan,Qiankun Wang,Bihao Guo,Chunwang Liu,Rui Yang,Yang Li,Yu Fan,Jiasheng Hu,Junhe Wang,Shuyue Zheng,Shengbin Wang,Xinfang Zhang,Feng Wu,Hantao Sun,Jianxin Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究利用AshN控制方案，通过在超导量子处理器中将逻辑相互作用与量子比特路由合并为单一原生操作，有效缓解了有限量子比特连接性的问题，显著减少了双量子门数量，并成功制备和验证了八量子比特的真正多体纠缠态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决超导量子处理器中由于平面布局导致的量子比特间连接性受限问题，减少因远距离量子比特相互作用所需的额外门操作。

Method: 采用AshN控制方案，在每个最近邻量子比特对上实现丰富的双量子比特控制，将逻辑相互作用和量子比特路由融合为一个原生操作，从而提升硬件图的连通性。

Result: 在多个基准电路中，相比传统的CZ基编译方法，AshN方案在一维和二维拓扑结构上分别实现了45.2%和43.7%的双量子门数几何平均减少；成功制备出保真度为0.736的八量子比特两激发Dicke态，并通过完全正偏转置见证者验证了其真正的多体纠缠特性，且结果在不同晶格配置（包括最多三个连接缺陷）下均保持稳健。

Conclusion: 原生门工程是一种有效缓解量子硬件连接性限制的实用方法，AshN控制方案为在受限架构上高效执行量子电路提供了新路径。

Abstract: Limited qubit connectivity is a central architectural constraint in superconducting quantum processors, whose planar layouts require additional gates to mediate interactions between distant qubits. Here, we use the AshN control scheme, where rich two-qubit control on every nearest-neighbour pair allows a logical interaction and the required qubit routing to be merged into a single native operation, effectively transforming a sparse hardware graph into a more connected computational architecture. For the benchmark instances studied, the resulting synthesis capability enables reliable execution on constrained one- and two-dimensional lattices, with compiled two-qubit gate counts approaching those of an all-to-all-connected reference. Across seven benchmark circuits on one- and two-dimensional topologies, the AshN-based implementation achieves geometric-mean reductions of $45.2\%$ and $43.7\%$ in two-qubit gate count compared with controlled-Z-based compilation, respectively. Using AshN gates, we prepare an eight-qubit two-excitation Dicke state with a fidelity of $0.736$ and certify its genuine multipartite entanglement using a fully positive-partial-transpose witness, whereas the same witness does not certify entanglement for the CZ-based implementation. The state fidelity and entanglement certification remain robust across the tested lattice configurations, including those with up to three connectivity defects. Our work establishes native-gate engineering as a practical approach to mitigating connectivity constraints.

</details>


### [126] [Quantum Secure Time Transfer for Satellites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24092)
*Ravi Singh Adhikari,Anju Rani,Aman Gupta,Xiaoyu Ai,Robert Malaney*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于纠缠光子的量子安全时间传递（QSTT）系统，在模拟低地球轨道卫星-地面信道中实现了比“墨子号”卫星高30倍的量子密钥分发（QKD）速率，并实现了无需GPS的时钟同步和后量子安全性，显著提升了空间高精度安全时间传递的效率与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现更高效、更安全的空间时间同步，克服现有卫星系统如Micius在QKD密钥率和依赖GPS等方面的局限性。

Method: 采用type-0 Sagnac结构的纠缠光子源，在模拟低地球轨道卫星到地面的信道中实验验证了新型QSTT系统，并结合预共享密钥实现系统配置混淆、嵌入后量子安全机制并优化QKD使用。

Result: 实现了约3比特/秒的有限QKD密钥率，安全参数为10^-10，相比Micius卫星系统提升了30倍；同时实现了无需GPS的时钟同步。

Conclusion: 该QSTT系统是迄今为止最高效且最安全的时间传递方案，为未来高精度、超安全的空间时间同步提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We experimentally demonstrate an entanglement-based Quantum-Secure Time Transfer (QSTT) system in an emulated low Earth orbit satellite-to-ground channel using a type-0 Sagnac-based entangled-photon source. Our new QSTT system delivers a finite Quantum Key Distribution (QKD) key rate of approximately $3~$bits$~\text{s}^{-1}$ with a $10^{-10}$ security parameter, providing a $30$-fold increase in the QKD key rate compared to the state-of-the-art entanglement-based QKD system delivered by the Micius satellite. Beyond this high key rate outcome, novel to our QSTT system is a GPS-free clock synchronization, optimized use of QKD, embedded post-quantum security, and obfuscation of the system configuration via use of a pre-shared key. Collectively, these enhancements deliver the most efficient and secure deployment of QSTT to date, and point the way forward to high-accuracy ultra-secure time transfer in space.

</details>


### [127] [Efficient Treatment of Non-Linearity in Quantum Computational Fluid Dynamics Using Hybrid Tensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24150)
*Pia Siegl,Nis-Luca van Hülst,Maximilian Mandelt Buxadé,Tomohiro Hashizume,Dieter Jaksch*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合量子-经典张量网络算法，用于高效求解计算流体动力学中的非线性项，实现了高成功概率和较低测量开销，展现出在大规模模拟中实现量子优势的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性项在量子计算流体动力学中难以处理，因量子硬件本质上线性，传统方法资源消耗大、可扩展性差。

Method: 结合变分时间步进与量子张量编程，采用基于张量的块编码替代传统的状态基非线性实现，在概率框架下编译算子和时变场到量子电路。

Result: 在湍流场基准测试中，算法在高雷诺数和细网格下保持高成功概率和适中测量开销；相比纯经典张量求解器，显著降低内存占用和计算成本。

Conclusion: 该混合算法为实现可扩展的大规模量子计算流体力学模拟提供了可行路径，推动了实际量子优势的实现。

Abstract: Nonlinear terms present a fundamental challenge for quantum computational fluid dynamics, as their implementation on inherently linear quantum hardware typically requires resource-intensive workarounds that limit scalability to large-scale simulations. We present a hybrid quantum-classical tensor network algorithm that addresses this bottleneck by combining variational time-stepping with quantum tensor programming to efficiently compile operators and time-dependent fields into quantum circuits. Within a probabilistic framework, we replace prior state-based nonlinear implementations with tensor-based block encodings, stabilizing success probabilities that otherwise decay exponentially with system size. Benchmarking on turbulent flow fields demonstrates that the algorithm maintains high success probabilities and moderate measurement overhead across increasing Reynolds numbers and grid resolutions. Compared to fully classical tensor network solvers, our hybrid approach yields substantial reductions in both memory footprint and computational cost, establishing a scalable pathway toward practical quantum advantage in scale-resolving CFD simulations.

</details>


### [128] [A Dynamic-Kernel/QPacket Executable for Quantum Repeater Chains in Q2NS/ns-3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24152)
*Adam Pearson,Marcello Caleffi,Angela Sara Cacciapuoti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并实现了基于动态内核/QPacket逻辑的量子互联网协议，用于在线性量子中继链上分发纠缠，并通过Q2NS/ns-3仿真平台验证了其在节点异构性和策略选择下的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子互联网依赖于纠缠这一非局域、不可复制的资源，传统分层协议难以适用，因此需要超越经典分层结构的新协议组织方式。

Method: 基于Q2NS（一个ns-3平台上的量子网络模拟模块），实现了一个可执行的QPacket协议特例，采用Planner--Executor--Engine架构的节点本地动态内核处理带有服务意图和仅追加操作戳的QPacket元头部，并结合链路准备策略支持前分布纠缠与非均匀生成能力。

Result: 仿真验证了链路可解析性模型，揭示了信令负载、转发行为和元头部增长情况；结果显示QPacket开销不仅受编码影响，还受到策略选择和网络资源可用性的影响。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了Q2NS/ns-3平台如何支持对量子原生协议概念进行可重复、特定策略的评估，推动超越传统分层架构的量子网络协议发展。

Abstract: The Quantum Internet operates on entanglement, a non-local, non-copyable, stateful network resource, which motivates protocol organization beyond classical layering. We present a first executable specialization of the Dynamic Kernel/QPacket logic from the beyond-layering protocol suite, targeting entanglement distribution over a linear quantum repeater chain. The implementation builds on Q2NS, an ns-3-based quantum-network simulation module available through the ns-3 App Store. It realizes QPacket meta-headers with service intent and append-only action-commit stamps processed by node-local Dynamic Kernels organized as a Planner--Executor--Engine pipeline, while being deliberately scoped to an analytically verifiable service and policy. Within this scoped setting, we study node heterogeneity through a link-preparation policy that accounts for pre-distributed entanglement and uneven entanglement-generation support across nodes, including delegation via QPacket forwarding. Simulations verify analytical link-resolvability models and expose signaling load, forwarding behavior, and QPacket meta-header growth. Results show that QPacket overhead is shaped by more than just encoding, including policy choices and available network resources. Overall, this study demonstrates how the Q2NS/ns-3 substrate can support reproducible, policy-specific evaluation of quantum-native protocol-suite concepts.

</details>


### [129] [A Theory of Finite-Noise Optima and Generalization in Quantum Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24229)
*Ziyu Zhang,Zikang Jia,Xiaosong Li,Yulong Dong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统计学习理论，通过引入噪声顺序纯度参数，解释了中等强度噪声下量子机器学习泛化误差降低的现象，并揭示了噪声对模型复杂度、泛化差距和预测偏差的综合影响，实现了对噪声非单调效应的预测与利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论无法解释中等噪声改善量子机器学习测试误差的现象，需要建立连接微观噪声过程与宏观学习性能的新理论框架。

Method: 基于代理模型分析，提出噪声顺序纯度参数，结合统计学习理论分析噪声对模型复杂度、泛化差距和预测偏差的影响，并通过数值实验验证理论预测。

Result: 发现中等噪声可通过降低模型复杂度来减小泛化差距，但同时增加预测偏差，二者竞争导致存在一个有限噪声最优值；该最优值依赖于学习设置，在大样本极限下可能消失；噪声编程可引导模型接近此最优状态。

Conclusion: 噪声对量子机器学习的影响具有非单调性，其效应可通过所提出的理论进行预测，并可通过噪声编程加以利用以优化学习性能。

Abstract: Quantum noise is expected to degrade quantum machine learning by driving circuits away from their noiseless implementations. Yet recent studies show moderate noise can reduce testing error, a behavior unexplained by weak-noise perturbative error accumulation or strong-noise trainability collapse. Here we develop a statistical learning theory connecting microscopic noise processes to macroscopic learning performance. At its heart is a noise-order purity parameter, derived from a surrogate model analysis, that predicts the noise-induced reduction in model complexity and the consequent reduction in the generalization gap. Noise simultaneously increases prediction bias. Their competition explains the intermediate-noise regime left open between these limits. It produces a finite-noise optimum whose location depends on the learning setup and can disappear in the large-sample limit. Numerical experiments validate these predictions. Noise programming can move a model towards this optimum. These results make the non-monotonic effect of noise predictable and provide a route to harness it.

</details>


### [130] [Three-slit interference with a which-path memory ancilla: A bright-dark state formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24243)
*Ajay Kumar,Guruprasad Kadam,Anirban Pathak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了路径记忆辅助对三缝干涉图样的影响，利用亮-暗态描述框架分析了暗子空间的内部量子结构，并探讨了多路径情况下路径相干性的损失机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在三缝干涉实验中，路径记忆辅助如何影响干涉图样，以及暗子空间的量子特性如何体现多路径干涉的独特性质。

Method: 采用亮-暗态理论框架，结合冯·诺依曼熵和相干性度量（如暗区相干性和亮-暗相干性），分析三缝系统中路径信息与量子相干性的关系。

Result: 发现两缝系统中亮-暗相干性仅由种群和单个双路径相干决定，而在三缝及以上系统中，其具有由路径间重叠不对称性引起的真正多路径贡献；同时区分了可恢复和不可逆的路径相干性损失。

Conclusion: 亮-暗相干性是基矢不变的物理量，能够反映多路径干涉中的内在量子结构，且多路径系统具有独特的相干性特征，不同于简单的双路径推广。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the effect of a which-path memory ancilla on the three-slit interference pattern within the framework of the bright--dark state description [Phys. Rev. Lett. 134, 133603 (2025)]. Whereas two slits give one bright mode, a photonic mode that couples to the detector atoms, and one dark mode, which does not couple to the detector atoms, three slits lead to one detector-coupled bright mode and a two-dimensional dark-mode subspace in the three-dimensional path space. We first discuss the classical three-slit interference pattern in terms of probability leakage into the dark subspace. We then study the von Neumann entropy $S_D$ of the dark subspace and the coherence measures of the reduced photonic state obtained by tracing out the memory: dark-sector coherence $C_D$ and bright--dark coherence $C_{BD}$. These quantities bring out the internal quantum structure of the two-dimensional dark subspace. We show that, whereas $C_D$ is not by itself a basis-independent physical observable, $C_{BD}$ is basis-invariant. We establish that, for two paths, $C_{BD}$ is fixed entirely by the populations and the single pairwise coherence, whereas for multiple paths, $M\geq 3$, it acquires a genuinely multipath contribution generated by asymmetry among the pairwise which-path overlaps. Finally, we distinguish the part of the path-coherence loss that is recoverable through a suitable measurement of the memory from the irreducible coherence deficit imposed by an uncontrolled environment.

</details>


### [131] [Distributed Resource Theory of Entanglement and Magic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24299)
*Xiao Yuan,Wenhao Zhang,Qiming Ding,You Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了分布式纠缠与魔法资源理论（DREAM），揭示了在量子网络中可通过无纠缠的分离态实现魔法态的远程传送，建立了纠缠与魔法统一的资源理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索分布式量子计算中纠缠与魔法这两种关键资源是否可以被统一描述，解决二者在传统框架下被独立处理的问题。

Method: 构建DREAM框架，将自由态定义为局部稳定子态的乘积混合态，自由操作为经典通信辅助的局部稳定子电路（LSCC），并分析其资源态与操作特性。

Result: 发现DREAM中存在既非纠缠也非魔法的非平凡资源态；实现了无需消耗纠缠即可通过分离态传送魔法态；推广至量子网络并揭示LSCC下的量子态传送能力与被传送态的魔法量密切相关。

Conclusion: DREAM为分布式量子资源提供了系统性研究框架，揭示了不同量子资源之间的内在联系，表明统一资源理论能揭示传统独立处理方法无法发现的新现象。

Abstract: In distributed fault-tolerant quantum computing, entanglement and magic are essential resources for quantum communication and universal fault-tolerant computation, respectively. Although they are usually treated as distinct resource currencies, whether they admit a unified resource-theoretic description remains an open question. Here, we introduce the distributed resource theory of entanglement and magic (DREAM). In this framework, the free states are convex mixtures of product local stabilizer states, and the free operations are local stabilizer circuits assisted by classical communication (LSCC). We show that DREAM contains nontrivial resource states that are neither entanglement nor magic, so it is strictly richer than treating the two resources independently. Surprisingly, such resources can enable the teleportation of magic states between distant parties without consuming entanglement, revealing a counterintuitive form of resource teleportation mediated entirely by separable states. We further generalize this result to quantum networks and investigate general quantum-state teleportation under LSCC. We show that the shared resource under DREAM is closely related to the teleportation capability, quantified by the magic of the teleported state. Our work establish a systematic framework for studying distributed quantum resources and uncover intrinsic relations among distinct resources within a unified resource theory.

</details>


### [132] [Essentially optimal gate teleportation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24345)
*Lukas Schmitt,David Sutter*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种确定性的LOCC协议，用于以接近最优的纠缠消耗实现两量子比特受控相位门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的确定性远距传送协议对所有门至少消耗一个ebit，但对于非Clifford门并非最优，特别是小角度相位门应能以更少纠缠实现。

Method: 设计了一种基于局域操作、经典通信和共享纠缠态的确定性门远距传送协议，专门针对受控相位门 $U_φ$，并优化其纠缠消耗。

Result: 该协议对任意 $φ∈[0,π]$ 实现受控相位门，且在小旋转角度下所需纠缠趋近于零，纠缠消耗接近理论最优。

Conclusion: 实现了对受控相位门的高效确定性远距传送，显著降低了非Clifford门尤其是小角度门的纠缠资源开销。

Abstract: Gate teleportation allows us to implement a nonlocal unitary using local operations, classical communication (LOCC), and a shared entangled state. Known deterministic teleportation protocols consume at least one full ebit and achieve optimal entanglement consumption only for Clifford gates. Here, we present a deterministic LOCC protocol for implementing the two-qubit controlled-phase gate $U_φ=\mathrm{diag}(1,1,1,e^{i φ})$ with $φ\in [0,π]$ whose entanglement consumption is close to optimal for every $φ$. In particular, vanishing rotation angles require vanishing entanglement.

</details>


### [133] [ABSTRACTS: Amsterdam Benchmark Suite for the Time and Resource Analysis of Clifford+T Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24370)
*Matthew Sutcliffe,John van de Wetering*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种标准化的基准测试套件和基础设施，用于量化非Clifford经典模拟器的效率，通过一致的电路数据集和虚拟硬件实现公平的结果比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献缺乏对非Clifford量子电路模拟方法进行标准化基准测试的方法，导致不同研究之间难以公平比较。

Method: 设计并实现了一个标准化的基准测试套件和基础设施，提供一致的电路数据集和虚拟硬件环境。

Result: 该基准测试套件能够支持不同非Clifford经典模拟器之间的公平效率比较。

Conclusion: 标准化的基准测试框架有助于推动非Clifford量子电路模拟技术的发展和评估。

Abstract: Recent years have seen a rapid growth in literature presenting new methods for simulating non-Clifford quantum circuits with classical hardware. These methods span a range of approaches, including stabiliser decomposition and tensor contraction techniques, varying in efficiency depending on circuit class, depth, non-Clifford gate count, and other metrics. A notable limitation of this literature is the lack of a standardised approach to benchmarking, with each new paper outlining its own specification, simulating its own set of circuits on the authors' own hardware. This paper seeks to address this issue by presenting a standardised and canonical benchmark suite and infrastructure for quantifying the efficiency of non-Clifford classical simulators, with a consistent dataset of circuits and providing consistent (virtual) hardware, thereby enabling a fair comparison of results.

</details>


### [134] [Towards Stirling cooler operable single-photon sources based on low-noise GaAs quantum dots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24409)
*Maximilian Aigner,Jana Schlücking,Eva Schöll,Christian Weidinger,Gabriel Undeutsch,Ievgen Brytavskyi,Thomas Oberleitner,Tobias Maria Krieger,Ailton Jose Garcia Junior,Melina Peter,Thomas K. Bracht,Michał Gawełczyk,Saimon Filipe Covre da Silva,Santanu Manna,Yusuf Karli,Gregor Weihs,Doris E. Reiter,Armando Rastelli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了GaAs量子点中负三子辐射衰变产生的光子在不同温度下的干涉特性，发现低温下光子不可区分性接近 unity，但随温度升高急剧下降；通过腔增强发射和 Purcell 效应，显著提高了较高温度下的光子不可区分性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现无需复杂冷却系统的高不可区分性光子源，推动室温可用的量子光子技术发展。

Method: 进行温度依赖的双光子干涉实验，并结合考虑载流子-声子相互作用和激发态耦合的理论计算；利用腔增强发射验证Purcell效应对提升不可区分性的效果。

Result: 在最低温度下，光子不可区分性达到0.966(6)；在55 K时降至0.05(4)；通过腔增强，在32 K下不可区分性从0.314(25)提升至0.80(3)，为该温度下最高值。

Conclusion: 激发态耦合是高温下不可区分性降低的主要原因，Purcell增强或增大激发态能级分离可有效改善高温性能，腔增强方法已被实验证实有效。

Abstract: For photonic quantum technology applications, sources capable of emitting photons with indistinguishability close to unity are essential. Ideally, these sources should not require demanding cooling systems. Here, we present temperature-dependent two-photon-interference measurements on photons produced by the radiative decay of the negative trion in a low-noise GaAs quantum dot, which are in quantitative agreement with theoretical calculations accounting for carrier-phonon interactions and coupling to excited states. While at at the lowest explored temperatures the emission linewidth reaches values only 6(2) % above the Fourier limit and the indistinguishability I between subsequently emitted photons reaches 0.966(6), the latter drops to 0.05(4) at 55 K. We show that this loss can be explained with the coupling with energetically close excited trion states and suggest that the photon indistinguishability at elevated temperatures can be increased by employing Purcell enhancement of the emission rate or by increasing the energy separation of the excited states. Using cavity-enhanced emission, we experimentally verify the first route and demonstrate an improvement in photon indistinguishability from 0.314(25) to 0.80(3) at 32 K, which - to our knowledge - is the highest reported value at such temperature.

</details>


### [135] [Nearly Optimal Amplitude Estimation at any Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24434)
*Jona Erle,Bálint Koczor*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一类无需辅助量子比特和受控Grover操作的振幅估计算法，具有可调电路深度和重复次数，实现了近似最优的查询-深度权衡，并在全范围内保持量子加速性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统振幅估计算法在边界区域性能退化，且需要辅助量子比特或复杂控制操作，限制了其在早期容错量子计算中的应用。

Method: 设计了具有可调电路深度M和重复次数N的振幅估计算法，通过避免使用辅助量子比特和受控Grover操作，实现对Grover角度误差的均匀控制。

Result: 实现了近乎最优的查询-深度权衡M²N∈Õ(ε⁻²)，在整个[0,π/2]区间内保持精度，包括边界区域；数值实验验证了理论预测的均匀角度精度和低实际开销。

Conclusion: 该算法在保持量子加速的同时适用于早期容错量子计算环境，为重叠认证、初态验证和蒙特卡洛方法等应用提供了实用化的振幅估计方案。

Abstract: We develop a class of amplitude estimation algorithms with tunable circuit depth $M$ and circuit repetitions $N$, requiring neither ancilla qubits nor controlled Grover operations. For additive error $ε$ in the Grover angle, they attain the nearly optimal query-depth tradeoff $M^2N\in\widetilde{O}(ε^{-2})$ uniformly over $λ\in[0,π/2]$, spanning the full range from classical sampling at $M=1$ to the Heisenberg limit at $M=Θ(ε^{-1})$. While prior depth-tunable work establishes comparable angle-accuracy guarantees only away from the boundaries $λ\to0$ or $π/2$ or at discrete depth tradeoffs, our guarantee extends to both boundaries, so the quantum speedup persists there rather than degrading to classical sampling. Numerical experiments confirm the predicted uniform angle accuracy and show low overhead in practice, making them strong candidates for practical amplitude estimation in the early fault-tolerant regime, with applications such as overlap certification, trial-state verification, and Monte Carlo methods.

</details>


### [136] [Tunable-Size Unruh-DeWitt Detector in a Multimode Cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24451)
*Simon Brunner,Farokh Mivehvar,Helmut Ritsch,Arkadiusz Kosior*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于多模光学腔的镊子捕获原子作为动量选择性的Unruh-DeWitt探测器，用于克服BEC中非声子激发导致相对论描述失效的问题，实现可调的有效探测器尺寸和增强信号响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（BEC）的量子模拟器中，传统探测器会耦合到声子区域以外的激发态，导致非线性Bogoliubov色散破坏有效相对论描述。为解决此问题，需发展一种能抑制非声子激发影响的新探测机制。

Method: 构建一个通过多模光学腔与BEC耦合的镊子捕获原子系统，利用腔介导的相互作用核使原子采样有限区域内密度涨落，形成等效有限尺寸探测器；通过调控腔模式结构实现动量截断，滤除非声子激发。解析计算了该探测器在匀加速直线运动和匀速圆周运动下的响应。

Result: 该方案实现了可调节的有效探测器尺寸，并通过动量选择性显著增强在实验可及加速度下的响应信号；兼容现有腔QED平台，适用于模拟量子场测量或相对论量子信息协议。

Conclusion: 所提出的动量选择性Unruh-DeWitt探测器模型能够有效克服BEC中非声子激发带来的限制，提升类比相对论效应的探测能力，为实验室研究相对论量子现象提供了新途径。

Abstract: Quantum simulators based on Bose-Einstein condensates (BECs) provide a powerful platform to study relativistic quantum-field phenomena in the lab and in particular emulate relativistic analog particle detectors. However, such detectors typically couple to excitations beyond the acoustic phonon regime, so that the nonlinear Bogoliubov dispersion introduces corrections that cause the effective relativistic description to break down. We propose to overcome this limitation using an intrinsically momentum-selective Unruh-DeWitt detector model in which a tweezer-trapped atom couples to the condensate through a multimode optical cavity. Although the atom is effectively pointlike, the cavity-mediated interaction kernel causes it to sample density fluctuations over a controllable finite region of the BEC equivalent to a finite-size detector. The effective detector size is tunable via the cavity mode structure determining a controllable momentum cutoff that filters out nonphononic excitations. We analytically calculate the response of such a detector for uniform linear acceleration and uniform circular motion and show that this momentum selectivity can enhance the signal at experimentally accessible accelerations. Compatible with existing cavity-QED platforms our scheme thus provides a versatile setting for analog quantum-field measurements or relativistic quantum-information protocols.

</details>


### [137] [Optimal copy complexity of quantum state cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24484)
*Sangwoo Jeon,Vaughn Sohn,Changhun Oh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子态克隆问题，确定了在d维、秩不超过r的混合态下最优渐近复制复杂度为N=Θ(Mrd/ε)，并通过随机纯化和纯态最优克隆实现了匹配的上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于纯态量子克隆已有精确结果，但对一般混合态尚无类似刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 利用固定平坦谱的态族证明下界，并通过随机纯化结合最优纯态克隆实现上界；对于单副本情况，将高保真层析相干转换为克隆。

Result: 得出了d维秩至多为r的混合态的最优渐近复制复杂度N=Θ(Mrd/ε)，且该下界在特定态族上成立，上界可通过随机纯化加纯态克隆实现。

Conclusion: 量子混合态的最优克隆复杂度与tomography复杂度具有相同的标度行为，揭示了其操作上的内在联系。

Abstract: Quantum state cloning is the task of approximately producing additional copies of an unknown quantum state from a finite number of input copies. The optimal cloning fidelity is known exactly for pure states, but no comparable characterization is known for general mixed states. We determine the optimal asymptotic copy complexity for $d$-dimensional states of rank at most $r$: producing $M$ additional copies with worst-case fidelity at least $1-\varepsilon$ requires and is achievable with $N=Θ(Mrd/\varepsilon)$ input copies. Remarkably, the lower bound already holds for a family of states with a fixed flat spectrum, while the matching upper bound is achieved by random purification followed by optimal pure-state cloning. For $M=1$, we further show that high-fidelity tomography can be coherently converted into cloning with comparable error, revealing an operational origin of the matching cloning and tomography complexities.

</details>


### [138] [Optimal Lower Bound for Ground-State Energy Estimation with a Guiding State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24493)
*Rolando D. Somma,Ronald de Wolf*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了引导哈密顿量问题中估计基态能量所需的U及其逆操作的应用次数，给出了具有紧致ε依赖性的联合下界Ω(log(1/ε)/γδ)，并在多种情况下验证了该下界的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了确定在给定引导态的情况下估计哈密顿量基态能量所需要的最少资源（即U和U⁻¹的应用次数），并填补此前关于参数ε、γ、δ联合依赖性下界理论的空白。

Method: 通过构造对抗性哈密顿量实例并结合信息论下界分析，证明在维度足够时存在Ω(log(1/ε)/γδ)的查询复杂度下界，并推广到有能隙、唯一基态、基态制备及块编码等情形。

Result: 证明了引导哈密顿问题的查询复杂度下界为Ω(log(1/ε)/γδ)，且该下界在多种物理相关设定下均成立；同时导出了非负哈密顿量表示为平方和时的改进下界Ω(log(1/ε)/γ√δ)。

Conclusion: 该结果确立了引导哈密顿问题中对误差、成功率和重叠幅度联合依赖的最优复杂度下界，完善了此前仅固定单一参数时的下界分析，表明现有上界在主要参数上已几乎紧致。

Abstract: The guided Hamiltonian problem is the following: given access to the unitary $U=e^{i H}$ for some Hamiltonian $H$, and given access to a unitary that prepares a guiding state promised to have overlap at least $γ>0$ with the ground space of $H$, estimate the ground-state energy of $H$ within additive error $δ> 0$ and success probability at least $1-\varepsilon $, $\varepsilon>0$. How many applications of $U$ and its inverse $U^{-1}$ are necessary and sufficient? An upper bound $O(\log(1/\varepsilon)\log(1/γ)/γδ)$ was known, and was improved to $O(\log(1/\varepsilon)/γδ)$ very recently [JW26]. A matching lower bound was known whenever one of the three parameters $δ,γ,\varepsilon$ was held constant [MdW26]. In this paper we prove the joint lower bound $Ω(\log(1/\varepsilon)/γδ)$ with the tight $\varepsilon$-dependence provided the dimension of $H$ is at least $\log(1/\varepsilon)/γ^2$. Furthermore, we show that this same lower bound (with slightly larger dimension) holds for both the special case in which the ground state is guaranteed to be unique and $H$ has a gap of $δ$ between its first and second eigenvalue; and for ground-state preparation, where $δ$ denotes the spectral gap and $\varepsilon$ now is the approximation error. The lower bounds also apply when the Hamiltonian can be accessed via its block-encoding, and when fractional powers of $U$ are allowed, as in continuous-time Hamiltonian simulation. Lastly, improved upper bounds are known when $H$ is nonnegative and presented as a sum of squares; and our results imply the lower bound $Ω(\log(1/\varepsilon)/γ\sqrtδ)$ for this case.

</details>


### [139] [Optimal Quantum Algorithm for Ground-State Energy Estimation with a Guiding State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24494)
*Stacey Jeffery,Freek Witteveen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的量子算法，利用转导器技术在引导状态下估计酉算子的最大特征相位，相较于之前的方法减少了log(1/γ)倍的查询次数，达到了已知下界并解决了Mande和de Wolf提出的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更高效地解决基态能量估计问题，特别是在给定引导态的情况下减少对哈密顿量演化算符的查询次数。

Method: 基于转导器（transducers）设计量子算法，使得在无需额外误差缩减开销的情况下组合量子算法，从而更高效地估计酉算子U的最大特征相位。
